# HANDOFF-NOTES: Tasks redesign (PC + shared model run)

Scope: PC + shared model only (bundle Phases 1-3 + the PC part of 7). Android UI and the Kotlin mirror of the model come in a later run; the phone keeps writing the OLD sync fields until then, so the PC must read the phone's old fields and map them into the new model on every sync.

Branch `tasks-ui-overhaul` (worktree `AccountabiliBuddy-tasks-ui`). Bundle: `doc/design-handoff-tasks/` (git-excluded, never stage). Questions: `HANDOFF-QUESTIONS.md`. Build: `"C:/Program Files/dotnet/dotnet.exe" build AccountabiliBuddy.sln` (log to `C:/Users/fblz/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/tasks-ui-logs/`, read only errors/tail).

## M2 must wire (done in M2 task 1, see above; kept for reference)

1. Task store (new doc, e.g. `tasks.items` + `tasks.labels` + `tasks.views` + `tasks.history`, DPAPI-sealed like the old lists) holding `SyncTaskItem`/`SyncTaskLabel`/`SyncTaskView`/`SyncTaskLogItem`; every local change through `TaskEdits.Edit` (title/note bases depend on it).
2. Startup migration: `LocalDatabase.BackupAsync("tasks-model")`, then `TaskMigration.Run` once behind marker `tasks.model` = 1; App maps `Comeback` -> `SyncParked` (ComebackStore.ForBackup shape, local items with text), `TaskFilterPreset`/`TaskViewPreferences` -> `LegacyTaskFilter`; store `ViewSearches` in a device-local document; log `Counts` (numbers only) and refuse to switch over unless `Counts.IsBalanced`. `IsNumberingDevice` from `TaskNumbering.NumberingDevice(ReceivedDevicesService.Active + self)`; `FirstNumber` = 1 on a fresh model.
3. SyncCoordinator write: `Tasks`/`Labels`/`Views`/`TaskLog` (through `SharedListMerge.ForFile`) plus `OldTaskFieldsWriter.Write(...)` for the old fields (replaces `NextTasks`/`Parked`/`Later`/`Categories`/`Comebacks` functions **and `AutoSaved` from `TaskListSync.Decorate`**); `Later` always written (even empty).
4. SyncCoordinator read: file with `Tasks` -> `TaskFieldMerge.Merge` (+ `SharedListMerge.Merge` for labels/views/taskLog); without -> `OldTaskFieldsReader.ReadFile` (it reads `AutoSaved` too, so `TaskListSync.ReceiveAsync` must stop merging `AutoSaved` into `AutoSavedTaskStore`); then `TaskNumbering.AssignFinal` on the numbering PC. Read one file at a time: move pairing only looks inside one file.
5. Old stores (`SessionTasksService` draft, `LaterStore`, `ComebackStore`, `AutoSavedTaskStore`) become views/facades of the task store; running checklist via `TaskSessionMapper` (old category ids in a running checklist are not label ids: keep the label, see mapper).
6. History pruning: keep entries `TaskHistoryEntry.Kept` (90 d) after `When` (UI may list 30 d); see questions 22 and 31.

## Findings that shape the plan (read first)

| # | Finding | Consequence |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Categories sync **by name + colour only** (`SyncCategory(Name, Color, ColorEditedAt)`; items carry `Category`/`CategoryColor` strings, no id). `02` §3.2 "same ids" cannot hold across devices. | Label id = deterministic GUID from lowercased name; key labels by name when reading old fields. |
| 2 | ADR 72 already shipped `Labels` (plain names, `##word`) and `LocalTo`/handovers on every item. New **tags** = old `Labels`; new **label** = old category; per-task `devices` overlaps `LocalTo`. | Map `Labels`→`tags`, `LocalTo`→`devices=[keeper]`; keep handover machinery until `devices` replaces it (Q8). |
| 3 | The draft (`_nextTasks`, doc `session.nextTasks`) is mirrored into `Current` (`session.tasks`) while no session runs; a running checklist is PC-owned and never merged. | `session` bin must equal `Current.Tasks` while live; remote actions keep going through `AddRemoteAsync`/`EditRemoteAsync`/`ReorderRemoteAsync`. |
| 4 | The 3-cap counts **tasks** today (`MaxTasks`, `TaskAllowance`, `NeedsPick`, `KeepOnlyAsync`, `PickTasksViewModel`, over-limit reasons in `session.overLimit`). Redesign caps **actions** (focused steps), setting 2-5. | Largest behavioural change; ADR 25 "All tasks done" must count actions. |
| 5 | New bin `next` (Shelf › Next) has no old home. | Dual-write puts it in old `later` and marks it with a new optional field so the reverse read does not turn Next into Later (see Data mapping). |
| 6 | Google scope is `calendar.readonly`. | Calendar export needs a broader scope: stop condition 1, skip (Q4). |
| 7 | No primary/owner PC concept exists. | Deterministic rule (Q3). |
| 8 | Next free ADR number here is 73, but the main checkout already holds 73-75. | Use 76 (Q1). |

| Task settings | `SessionTasksService.MaxTasks = 3` (const), `NotifyAllDone` (in `session.tasks`) | new document `tasks.settings` (device-local prefs) + synced bits via `SyncDocument` where `SyncFile.Documents` already carries settings |
| Sync file | `Core/Sync/SyncFile.cs` (`NextTasks`, `Parked`, `Later`, `Comebacks`, `Categories`, `TaskHistory`, `PastSettled`, `AutoSaved`, `Handovers`, `HandoverAcks`), `SyncFileBuilder.cs`, `SyncFileCodec.cs`, `SyncMerge.cs`, `Services/Sync/SyncCoordinator.cs` (writes ~l.262-280, reads ~l.472-520) | add optional `Tasks`, `Labels`, `Views`, `TaskHistory2` names (Q9); format stays 1 |

## Data mapping (migration, `02` §3)

Run once, behind `LocalDatabase.BackupAsync("tasks-model")` (Core/Storage; whole-DB copy, covers the `documents` table), idempotent via a marker document (`tasks.model` = 1). No SQLite schema bump (stays 6), no profile bump (stays v13): task lists are app documents.

| Old store (document) | Old field | → New field |
|---|---|---|
| `session.categories` | `TaskCategory.Id/Name/Color` | `TaskLabel.id` = deterministic GUID of lowercased name (Q2), name, color; `ringOn` null; position by list order |
| `session.nextTasks` (draft, `SyncNextTask`) | `Id` | `id` |
| | `Title` | `title` |
| | `DoneAt` | `doneAt`; bin `done` if done today else history `finished` |
| | `Category`+`CategoryColor` (names) | `label` (by name), colour kept as label colour if label missing |
| | `Subtasks` (`SessionSubtask`) | `steps` (`focus` = first open steps filling free slots when bin `session`) |
| | `EstimateMinutes`, `Note` | `estimateMinutes`, `note` |
| | `PlannedFor` (`PlannedSlot`) | no target field: keep in a side field `plannedFor` (optional) so "Put in plan" survives; Q7 |
| | `Labels` (ADR 72) | `tags` |
| | `LocalTo` | `devices=[keeper]` (Q8) |
| | `EditedAt`/`DeletedAt`/`Position` | stamps/`position`; first 3 actions → `session`, rest `queued`, in order |
| `session.later` (`SyncLaterItem`) | all above + `DueOn` | bin `later`; `dueOn`; `DoneAt` set → history `finished` |
| `capture.comebacks` (`Comeback`/`SyncParked`) | `Text`, `CreatedAt`, `DoneAt`, `Category*`, `Labels`, `LocalTo` | title, `createdAt`, bin `sort`, origin "Come back to"/"Parked <date time>"; ticked ones → history `finished` |
| `tasks.autoSaved` | `Title`, `WrapUpOf`, `SavedAt`, `Labels` | bin `sort`, origin "Auto-saved <weekday m/d>" |
| `session.tasks` (running checklist) | `SessionTask` | unchanged while live; on next start the draft path applies |
| `session.taskHistory`, `tasks.keptPast`, `tasks.receivedHistory`, `tasks.pastSettled` | all | untouched |
| `tasks.filterPresets` / `tasks.view` | presets | `views` (name, cfg); `tasks.view` → default view `cfg` |
| all | `number` | assigned in `createdAt` order from the numbering device (Q3); `touchedAt`=`EditedAt`; `tags`=[] (or old `Labels`); `highlight`=false; `origin` per row above; `calendar`=setting default |

**Reverse mapping (dual-write for one release, and read of the phone's old fields).** Old fields are the phone's only channel until the Android run. Where read/written: write `Services/Sync/SyncCoordinator.cs` ~l.262-280 (functions `NextTasks`, `Parked`, `Later`, `Categories`, `Comebacks` wired at `App.axaml.cs` ~l.757-763, 954); read `SyncCoordinator.cs` ~l.472-520 (`MergeNextTasks`, `MergeParked`, `MergeLater`, categories via `SessionTasksService.AdoptCategoryAsync`/`OtherCategories`); ADR 72 parts via `TaskListSync.Decorate/ReceiveAsync`.

| Sync-file field (C# type, `Core/Sync/SyncFile.cs`) | Writer today | New-model projection (write) | Phone edit → new model (read) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `NextTasks` `IReadOnlyList<SyncNextTask>?` | `NextTasksForSync` | bins `session`+`queued`+`done`(today) ordered by position; `Category`/`CategoryColor` from label (opaque `#RRGGBB`, rgba dropped), `Labels` from tags, steps from `steps` | item-level newest `EditedAt` wins per field-group; unchanged fields inherit PC copy (ADR 50 `knowsDetails` pattern, extended: writer lacks `Tasks` ⇒ keep PC's reminders/highlight/due/number/devices) |
| `Later` `IReadOnlyList<SyncLaterItem>?` (always written, even empty, so phones see `knowsDetails`) | `LaterStore.ForSync` | bins `later` **and `next`** (new optional `Bin` marker on the item, Q10), `DueOn`, `DoneAt` from history `finished` of last 90 d | `later` item whose task is in `next`/`later` keeps its bin unless item `EditedAt` > bin stamp and no marker; moved lists = move bin |
| `Parked` `IReadOnlyList<SyncParked>?` (non-null = "has shared lists") | `ComebackStore.ForSync` | bin `sort` tasks | new item → bin `sort`; tick → `done` |
| `Comebacks` `IReadOnlyList<SyncComeback>?` (legacy, open only) | `SyncCoordinator` ~l.262 | open `sort` tasks | ignored when `Parked` present |
| `Categories` `IReadOnlyList<SyncCategory>?` (name+colour) | `syncCoordinator.Categories` (`App.axaml.cs` 954) | labels (name, opaque colour) | via `AdoptCategoryAsync(name,color)`; `TaskCategoryColors.TakeNewer` |
| `Actions` `IReadOnlyList<SyncAction>?` (`taskAdd`/`taskEdit`/`taskReorder`/`tickTask`/`park`/`tickComeback`, `SyncTaskRequest`) | phone | n/a | handled by `SessionTasksService.AddRemoteAsync/EditRemoteAsync/ReorderRemoteAsync` + `SyncActionsService`; cap enforced here (actions, not tasks) |
| `Running`/`SyncSession.Tasks` `IReadOnlyList<SessionTask>?` | `TasksOf` | focused steps as before | read-only on phone |
| `AutoSaved` `IReadOnlyList<SyncAutoSavedItem>?` | today `TaskListSync.Decorate` from `AutoSavedTaskStore`; **from M2 `OldTaskFieldsWriter` only** (drop it from `Decorate`, or the phone sees items twice) | open `sort` tasks with `AutoSavedFor` within 30 d of `CreatedAt` (they are then in no other old list; after 30 d they move to `parked`) | `OldTaskFieldsReader`: edits and tombstones by id; delete + new Later id = a move (paired) |
| `TaskHistory` `IReadOnlyList<SyncTaskHistoryEntry>?`, `PastSettled`, `Handovers`, `HandoverAcks` | `TaskListSync.Decorate` | unchanged | unchanged |
| **new, optional** `Tasks`, `Labels`, `Views` | PC (new builds) | full model, field stamps | new builds read these first, fall back to old fields |

Rules for the dual path: (1) old fields are always rebuilt from the task store on write, never stored separately; (2) on read, a file with `Tasks` is authoritative for that device; a file without it is mapped item by item with field-level "unchanged by old writer" inheritance; (3) tombstones: old `DeletedAt` → task tombstone; (4) never delete a new-model field because an old item lacks it.

## Numbering device

None exists. `Core/Sync/SyncDeviceCard.cs` (DeviceId, Name, Kind `pc`/`phone`/`watch`, AppVersion, WrittenAt, Methods, HistoryOff, Seen, Companions, NextAlarm, PublicKey) and `Services/Sync/ReceivedDevicesService.cs` have no primary/owner flag; ADR 61 is "What syncs", not ownership. Use `02` §4's rule: the PC with the lowest `Device.DeviceId` among PCs seen in the group (`ReceivedDevicesService.Active` + self), a phone-only group numbers on the phone (the PC run: temporary numbers only). Q3.

## Calendar

`Services/GoogleCalendarClient.cs` `Scope = "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/calendar.readonly"` (used by `CalendarSyncService.cs`, the user's own OAuth client; scopes listed in `GoogleSetupSteps.cs`): **read-only, cannot create calendars or events**. Export needs `calendar.events`/`calendar` (broader scope, new consent): stop condition 1, so do not build the Google export or the "AB Reminders" calendar; keep the setting hidden or disabled. Windows Calendar (WinRT appointments) is not referenced anywhere in the code today.

## Open risks for M1 (also see questions)

- Lossless migration: back up first; verify counts per old store vs new bins; keep old documents (`session.nextTasks`, `session.later`, `capture.comebacks`, `tasks.autoSaved`) in place for one release as the rollback source.
- Dual path correctness is the data-loss risk: phone edits an old item whose task has reminders/steps focus/highlight that the old type cannot carry.
- Cap by actions touches `SessionTasksService` (1.6k lines + 3 partials), `PickTasksViewModel`, `AddRemoteAsync`, `NeedsPick`, ADR 25 end-at-once, over-limit reasons.
- Hard-rules/ADR updates required in M1: `doc/hard-rules.md` (new fields, what each may carry), ADR 76.

## M1-M2 review cleanup (commit "Tasks (PC): M1-M2 review cleanup")

- Migration: a live duplicate's losing title/note is kept as a `TaskConflict` on the winner; ADR 76 notes the Next/Later creation-time and single-stamp limits.
- Numbering: a kept temporary number is skipped when another task's FormerNumbers holds it.
- Migration backup is written once per model version (`tasks.modelAttempt`, a state key); a failed migration shows a notice at start.
- Backup restore of `tasks.categoryIds` / `tasks.viewSearches` goes through `TaskStore.RestoreDeviceMapAsync`.
- Undo restores only the fields the action changed (`TaskUndoEntry.Fields`); tidy-up re-choice restores only the Bin field. Undoing a Bring back re-adds its Recently deleted entries (`RecentlyDeletedStore.ReinstateAsync`).
- Old-list rules use the running session's `ActionCap`.
- Q52: `TaskListRules.StaleDeletes` + `TaskStore.Notice` event + `TaskToasts.MovedElsewhere`; no UI yet (a later milestone shows it).
- Item 9 checked, no change: a ticked Later item/come-back files Finished; deleting it afterwards removes it from History by design (`CloseFinished`).

## M1-M2 milestone review fixes (commit "Tasks (PC): M1-M2 milestone review fixes")

- QR files (`SyncFileBuilder.ForQr`) drop `Tasks`, `Labels`, `Views`, `TaskLog`.
- `TaskViewService.MirrorPresetsAsync` runs each mirror on the UI thread after the previous one (`Dispatcher.UIThread.InvokeAsync`). Audit: the other TaskStore writers already run there (sync merge via `SyncCoordinator.AcceptSealedAsync` → `UIThread.InvokeAsync`, remote actions via `UIThread.Post(syncActions.ApplyAsync)`, backup import from the VM, the lists and VMs themselves).
- Undo skips what changed since (Q75).
- `SessionEngine.PauseTimer` refused outside focus and planning; step away in a break never freezes (Q74, ADR 77).
- `SessionTasksService.SwapOutAsync`: the checklist without the task is worked out after `PutBackAsync` (`MoveOutOfChecklistAsync` takes a function; nothing is written when the task already left).
- `TaskModelMigration`: a marker that can't be read → `Unreadable` (never the old lists on a guess).
- `TaskStore.SessionBin()` and `RunningChecklist` removed (no callers); `TaskStore.IsHeld` added; `SaveRunningAsync` goes through the `_saving` chain.
- Unreadable task list: `ComebackStore.AddAsync` / `AutoSavedTaskStore.AddAsync` refuse and raise `AddRefused`; the app shows a notice (Ctrl+Alt+N, the work gate's come-back, the shutdown card, every other add).
- `taskStore.SessionActions` is the setting again (the list's This session bin is always the next session's draft); the running checklist keeps its own `ActionCap` (replaces "Old-list rules use the running session's ActionCap" above).
- `tasks.tidyUp` classified as a state key.
- Re-check fixes: undoing a Bring back reinstates its Recently deleted entries after the restore, only for tasks not skipped (`TaskUndoRules.ToReinstate`). A task ticked done (another device) while `MoveOutOfChecklistAsync` puts it back stays done in the checklist (no drop) and leaves the list again (`TaskStore.TakeBackAsync`: held again, tombstoned, no History entry).

## M3 task 2 done (side-task timer setting; services/engine only, UI is M5/M7)

- Owner's decision (2026-11-11): "When I start a side task" = Keep timer running (default) / Pause timer, per mode in `tasks.settings`(`.pb`) (`TaskSettings.SideTask`, `PendingSideTask`, `PendingSideTaskFrom`). Same rules as "When I step away": `TaskSettingsService.SetSideTaskAsync` (looser waits, refused during a session; stricter at once), `SideTaskNow` (stricter of `SessionState.SideTaskAtStart` and now), restore via `RestoreAsync`, not in the compare view.
- Engine: `SessionEngine.StartSideTask(length, keepTimerRunning)` sets `AwayTimerRuns`; in a break it never freezes (Pause -> `AwayPausesInFocus`, frozen from the focus's start by `FrozenIfAwayAtFocus`). `RememberSideTask` at every session start (`SessionController.SideTaskSetting`). `StepAwayService.SideTaskSetting` (wired to `SideTaskNow`) picks the mode; `CheckNow` checks a side task's end first so its card/chime still come with the timer running.
- M7 settings UI: add a "When I start a side task" row next to "When I step away" calling `SetSideTaskAsync` (show the pending wait like the step-away row). Not added to SetupFeatures (no UI yet).
- Docs: ADR 0077 Side tasks bullet, `doc/hard-rules.md` side-task sentences, HANDOFF-QUESTIONS 85-87 (Q74's flag closed).
- Checks: `tasks-ui-checks` Program.cs (M3.2 block; 454/454 pass) + new `tasks-ui-checks/AppChecks` (App-level `TaskSettingsService`/`SessionController` on a temp DB; 20/20 pass). Dev app started once on dev data: no exceptions.

## M3 task 3 fix round (review of 1cbba319..bef5b000 + owner's item 9)

- Shared event ids: `TaskCalendarActionKind.Forget` + `TaskCalendarMapping.KeptElsewhere(Ids)`; `TaskCalendarDiff.Plan(wanted, recorded, keptElsewhere)` forgets (no DELETE) a task that still wants its event but isn't kept on this PC.

- `AllowAsync` re-reads the source after sign-in (disconnected meanwhile -> refused); `IsAppCalendar` by recorded id only.
- Item 9: `TaskDateOnlyEvent` + `TaskReminderSettings.DateOnlyEvent` ("Tasks with a date but no time": Timed at 9:00 AM default | All-day event); M7 adds the row next to "Add new reminders to".
- Docs: ADR 78, hard-rules (headline + ADR 78 entry), Q83. Checks: `CalendarExportChecks` 80/80.

## M4 milestone review fixes

- [x] 1 (blocking) `TaskActionsService.MoveToSessionAsync`: a task the running checklist took (`AddToTodayAsync` → Added while a session runs) leaves the list with `TaskStore.TakeBackAsync(_session, id)` (held for the session, no History entry); it was in This session and still on the Shelf / in Queued. Every page path goes through here: the Queued row's target and menu, drag into This session, the Shelf rows' drag. (The old "Add to Today" menu paths keep their own source removal.)
- [x] 2 (blocking, Q126 closed) `SessionTasksService.DraftActions` counts This session's draft tasks only; `HasDraftRoomFor` = `DraftActions() + actions <= ActionCap`. A Shelf task into This session with no session running now checks its `ActionCount` the same way (it only checked for one free action).
- [x] 3 (blocking, Q48 closed) `StartChecklistAsync` starts with `ThisSessionDraftTasks` only; Queued's tasks stay in the list (not held, not tombstoned: `TombstoneNextTasksAsync(joining)`), so the pick sees the same set (Q128 for the phone's old field).
- [x] 4 A Queued task ticked during a session shows under Finished today (row kind Queued, ticked; untick = `ReopenAsync`; its open task is read only) and never joins the next session as done (Q127). Its "Move to This session" menu entry shows only while it is in Queued.
- [x] Nits: pending ticks keyed by task in `TaskFinishQueue` (new), flushed on Quit (Q129); `_shelfRows` lists of groups no longer shown are dropped; the stale comment above `IsInDraft` is gone.

## M5 conflict-check fixes (review of dbce298d)

- [x] Ctrl+Z after a "Changed on both devices" pick: `TaskUndoStack.Forget(id)` drops every undo entry that touched the task once `TaskActionsService.ResolveConflictAsync` / `KeepBothVersionsAsync` saved the pick (an undo would have put back the pre-edit text stamped newest, losing both versions).
- [x] Keep both keeps the task's devices on the copy (`Devices: source.Devices`, ADR 72): a local or limited task's copy no longer syncs everywhere.
- [x] Merge (`TaskFieldMerge.PickText`, conflict branch): the shown version keeps its stamp and takes the other version's bases (`WinnerFirstBases`: winner's all kept, loser's fill the rest of the 10-value ring, the loser's own value never), so a stale in-between copy from a third device no longer re-raises a resolved conflict. Symmetric. Q173.
- [x] Nits: the conflict dialog rebuilds its cards when a sync changed the shown value with the conflict still open, shows the title's conflict before the note's and reads "2 of 2" after the first pick; a blank other title toasts "Kept this version"; `TaskViewService.EditAsync` leaves `Working` alone when the view was deleted elsewhere.
- Checks: Program.cs 542/542 (14 new m5conf: S2-S4, ring cap, a loser value in its own bases, 300 random three-device symmetry rounds); AppChecks 39/39 (4 new m5conf: rename, phone conflict, pick, Ctrl+Z leaves the pick; fails without the fix). Build 0 errors.
- Found, not fixed: with no session running, `UndoAsync` skips every Queued (and Session) task, because `SessionTasksService.IsInChecklist` also matches the draft checklist (which mirrors Queued + This session through the old `nextTasks` mapping): undoing an edit to a Queued task outside a session says "Can't undo · it changed since". The new AppCheck uses a Later task for that reason.

## For the Android run

Collected from these notes and HANDOFF-QUESTIONS (the phone keeps writing the old fields until then; the PC reads them and keeps dual-writing them).

1. Kotlin mirror of the model: `TaskItem` and friends (bins, steps + letters, labels with `ringOn`/rgba, tags, reminders + occurrences, views, History log), `SyncTaskItem` + field stamps, the optional parts `tasks`/`labels`/`views`/`taskLog` (lenient read, cleaned like `SyncFileCodec`), and the same migration as `TaskMigration` (balanced counts, label id from the lowercased name; Q2, Q39).
2. Kotlin `TaskFieldMerge` twin, field by field, including the conflict branch's winner-first bases union (`WinnerFirstBases`: the winner's bases kept, the loser's fill the 10-value ring, never the loser's own value; Q173) and the 2-minute clamp; Keep both copies `Devices` (M5 conflict fixes).
3. `taskAccountSettings` part: default view, picked calendar and `ringOn` + `ringOnStamp`, merged field by field (`TaskAccountSettingsMerge`, adopted stamps older than any choice; Q241); a phone rings only when both the account's and the label's lists name it (null = every device; Q242; PCs Q243).
4. Per-occurrence reminder merge (Q80): today the whole `reminders` field's newest stamp wins on both sides; changing it needs both twins at once. Acting on a reminder on the phone must stop it on the PC and back (08 Reminders).
5. `awaySince` with the running-timer step-away mode in `SyncRunning` (Q60).
6. "End and mark the rest unfinished": the phone's own "used ids" rule against `PastSettled` "moved" and the returned Queued tasks (Q170, Q174).
7. Numbering: a phone-only group numbers on the phone; device ids compared as lower-case text (Q3, Q26); the PC keeps temporary numbers meanwhile.
8. Per-task `devices` with 2+ entries (old fields can say only "everywhere" or one keeper; Q24) and the handover flow folded into `devices` (Q8, Q145).
9. Old fields: `nextTasks` during a PC session (Q128), the `later` item's `bin` marker for Next (Q10), `autoSaved` only from `OldTaskFieldsWriter`; drop the dual-write one release after the phone writes the new parts.
10. The session cap in actions from the phone (`AddRemoteAsync` already enforces it; the phone UI should show over-cap adds in Queued).
11. Snooze shortcuts: the spec syncs them; the PC keeps them device-local in `tasks.reminderSettings` (Q76, Q157). Decide one home for both.
12. Phone UI (Phases 4-6): the phone screens and sheets (two heights, From your lists, the filter sheet footer, draggable scrollbar), swipe targets, hold → lift, totals card, the full-screen alarm over the lock screen with "Open task" / "Show →" (Q192), Reminders due now, the permission banner, Sync card and conflict sheet, tablet two columns.
13. Phase 7-Android: Settings › Tasks, the Labels screen, Alerts › Task reminders (+ silent/DND, During a focus session with the same looser-change rule, Q254) and Rings on.
14. Phase 8-Android: phone SetupFeatures (`android/app/.../setup/SetupFeatures.kt`): Tasks redesign + the view chooser (same three choices, Q257), Task reminders incl. exact alarms / full-screen permission, ringing devices (13s picker, also when linking a new device).
15. 08's phone-only items: phone tabs without counts, sheets' back arrow and heights, swipe, tablet pop-ups, `./gradlew assembleDebug`.
16. Rings on (M7 label sync fixes, Q262-Q265): the Kotlin `labels` twin carries `ringOn` + `ringOnStamp` and merges rings on by its own stamp after the item merge (`TaskLabelMerge`), cleans it like `SyncFileCodec.CleanRingOn`; lists name phones only (`TaskRingDevices.Toggled`); the phone's ring check is `TaskRingDevices.RingsFor`. Until the phone writes the `labels` part, the PC keeps ignoring its old categories for labels deleted or renamed since (unless their colour was chosen after); once it does, the phone should drop its own categories list. The Kotlin label merge must also keep label tombstones for good and give a revived label `ringOn = null` with a ring stamp at the revive (label delete fixes).
17. Step away with Keep timer running (R2 review, Q354): the PC sends `awaySince` only when it froze the countdown, so with Keep timer running the phone never learns the user stepped away and can't offer I'm back. Needs a synced "stepped away" flag in `SyncRunning` (a hard-rules / ADR 42 change) before the phone's Session sheet can show it.

### R4 T1 done (goals model and maths, Core only; plan R4.2, R4.5, R4.6 rule)

- **Model** (`src/AccountabiliBuddy.Core/Goals/`, one public type per file, JSON through `SyncJson.Options`: camelCase, enums by name, nulls left out): `GoalArea`, `Goal` (+ `Goal.DeepWorkId`, `SecondTarget` = Deep work's weekly target), `GoalKind`, `GoalTarget` (+ `GoalPeriod`, `GoalUnit`, `GoalDirection`, `GoalPendingChange`, `missesAllowed` Q361), `GoalSourceRule` (+ `GoalSourceType` incl. `FocusAll`, `GoalBeeminderDirection`), `GoalSources.AvailabilityOf` (+ `GoalSourceAvailability`: GoalField / MilestoneTasks / HealthWeight Coming soon; FocusTag, Calendar, Beeminder PC only; sleep, screen time phone only), `GoalEntry` (+ `GoalEntrySource`), `GoalReminder` (+ `GoalReminderDays`), `GoalReviewRecord` (+ `GoalReviewDecision`, `GoalReviewChoice`), `GoalPauseStretch`, `GoalExtraTiles`, computed `GoalDayState` (+ `GoalDayStatus`). Areas, goals, entries, reminders and reviews carry id / position / editedAt / deletedAt like ADR 48/72 items (no sync code; ADR 79 stays Proposed). Private text (names, notes, reasons, screen-time app) is marked in the docs; sealing is R4.3's store.
- **Attribution rule (plan §3b 5), exactly:** all of a session's counted focus counts toward a label goal when any task of its checklist carries that label (category id, or `TaskLabel.IdFor` its name), and toward a #tag goal when any task carries the tag; no checklist → only all focus. Synced pieces: a checklist-less piece continues the same device's previous piece when neither is hand-added, same profile name, it starts at/after that piece's end, and `offMinutesBefore` is set or the gap ≤ 60 min. Data used: `SyncSession` (start, end, profile, added, tasks, offMinutesBefore) and its `SessionTask` (categoryId, category, labels) only; this PC's own sessions from `SessionRecord` + the checklist by start. Counted focus = the guards' rule (`FocusDayBuilder.CountedRecords` + new internal `DeepWork.SessionWindows`, behaviour unchanged) plus hand-added sessions (goals only). Q360, Q363.
- **Maths:** `GoalFocusAttribution` (+ `GoalFocusSession`), `GoalSourceData`, `GoalCounting` (day values per kind, union of time sources, hand edit wins, first reading, back-fill flag), `GoalPeriods` (+ `GoalPeriodSpan`, `GoalWeekStart` Sun default / Mon / Sat), `GoalStatus` (`ThisPeriod`, `ThisPeriodSpan`, `Period` → `GoalPeriodResult`, `Target` → `GoalTargetProgress`), `GoalStreak`, `GoalStrip14` (+ `GoalStripCell`, `GoalStripCellKind`), `GoalAge`, `GoalHeat`, `GoalWhatItTakes` (+ `GoalWhatItTakesVerdict`, `FriendlyMinutes`, `MinutesText`, `Compute` for the New goal box), `GoalReviewSuggestion`, `GoalLowering` (+ `GoalChangeDecision`, `GoalChangeOutcome`). Time comes in as `today` / `now` + `TimeZoneInfo` (TrustedClock stays App-side). Rules and readings: Q360–Q372.
- **Checks** (out of repo): new `tasks-ui-checks/GoalsChecks/` (Core only; `TasksUiChecks.csproj` now excludes the folder): **160/160** (attribution, once per goal, overlap across devices / calendar / hand time, pieces, hand-added vs guard, tags on/off, PC records = DeepWork's total; day statuses per kind; pause; streaks incl. today and pauses; strip = streak; What it takes met / behind / covered / no date / past date / rounding; lowering waits vs raising vs PB vs other goals, CanApply; back-fill → week + streak, guard unchanged; week start Sun/Mon/Sat, two weeks, month; age units; review Adjust / Pause / Keep going / late projection; heat; JSON shape). Others still pass: Core 642, AppChecks 274, AccountSettingsChecks 68, CalendarExportChecks 123. Solution build 0 errors (`tasks-ui-logs/r4t1-build.log`).
- **Parity fixtures** for the Kotlin mirror (R4.7): `%TEMP%/claude/round2-fixtures/goals/` — 15 files, 125 cases, written by GoalsChecks (synced shape only: `devices` = one `sessions` array per device, `goal`, `entries`, `zoneOffsetMinutes` −300, `today`, `now`, `weekStart`, `tagsAvailable`; snapshot cases expect day states with heat, status, this period, streak, strip, age, target, what it takes and review; plus `lowering`, `lowering-apply`, `periods`, `rounding`, `age`). Doubles are written as C# prints them: compare with a tolerance on the Kotlin side.
- **Left for R4:** the store (R4.3: documents, DPAPI sealing, `DeviceSettingsRegistry`, backup), the Deep work migration (R4.4), wiring `GoalLowering` into `GoalService`'s callers and the weekly target's pending change (R4.6), the Kotlin mirror (R4.7, against these fixtures). ### R4 T2 done (goals store, Deep work migration, lowering rules; App only, no UI; plan R4.3, R4.4, R4.6, §3b 6)

- **Store** `Services/Goals/GoalStore.cs` (+ `GoalLists`, `GoalModelState`, `GoalStoreChangedEventArgs`, `GoalUiPreferences` + `GoalListView`, `GoalPageSettings` + `GoalHeatmapLabels`, `DeepWorkGoalSettings`, `GoalDefaults`): five DPAPI-sealed lists `goals.items` / `areas` / `entries` / `reminders` / `reviews` (SyncJson inside, one `{data}` blob each, saved together in one transaction, saves queued in order); plain device-local `goals.deepWork` (weekly target, Track time only, waiting changes), `goals.ui` (folded areas, list width 360–560 / 470, view By area / Current / All), `goals.settings` (week start Sun, heatmap labels Some (Q373), extra tiles off); marker `goals.model` = 1, `goals.modelAttempt`. Deep work is kept whole on every change (`GoalDefaults.Normalized`: built in, Time, source FocusAll only, never paused or deleted, targets as views); 30-day tombstones pruned. `Changed` event with source / from backup. Not ready → every change refused; Unreadable never falls back (`goals.deepWork` unreadable counts as unreadable, Q379).
- **Migration** `Services/Goals/GoalsMigration.cs` (App, right after the task model block): marker → load path (unreadable → Unreadable); else `BackupAsync("goals")` once per model version, `Seed` (Work area + Deep work where missing, nothing else; fixed ids `GoalDefaults.WorkAreaId` / `DeepWorkSourceId`, Q378), `IsBalanced` (nothing lost, no id twice, one live built-in goal in a live area), then lists + marker in one transaction. Any failure: no marker, nothing written over, goals unavailable, everything else unchanged, retried next start. Log `goals-model.log`, counts only. Dev data (`%LocalAppData%\AccountabiliBuddy-Dev`): first start made 1 area + 1 goal behind `before-goals-…db`; second start took the load path.
- **Rules** `Services/Goals/GoalsService.cs` (+ `GoalChangeResult`, `DeepWorkTargetKind`), exposed as `App._goals` for R5: `DeepWork` = the stored record with the daily target from `GoalService` (the `goal` document stays the source, ADR 29) and the weekly one from `goals.deepWork`, each with its waiting change; `SetDeepWorkDailyAsync` (30–480, written through `GoalService.SetAsync`), `SetDeepWorkWeeklyAsync` (30–3360 or none), `SetTrackTimeOnlyAsync`, `CancelDeepWorkPendingAsync`, `ApplyPendingIfReadyAsync` (every session tick), `ModeChangedAsync` (App: `guidance.Changed`), `RestoreDeepWorkAsync`; other goals: `AddGoalAsync`, `UpdateGoalAsync`, `PauseGoalAsync` / `ResumeGoalAsync`, `DeleteGoalAsync`, areas, entries, reminders, reviews, `SetUiPreferencesAsync`, `SetPageSettingsAsync`. Lowering Deep work = `GoalLowering.Decide` + the `TaskSettingsService` pattern: outside PB refused in a session, else waits the looser-change delay (kept in the document), applies with no session and the work-hours rule clear; PB per Q362 (no delay, no session rule, work-hours rule kept); switching into AB restarts the delay (Q377); raising at once and drops the wait; a daily goal set elsewhere drops a waiting lower value (Q375). Track time only never changes the `goal` document (Q374). Deep work: no pause, delete, hand entry or other source. Other goals: at once in both modes; "Coming soon" sources refused.
- **Guard safety (§3b 6), verified by reading:** `DeepWorkService` (Progress, `UnlockProgress` = the work-hours unlock, `FocusedOn` = Budget / guards' counted focus) reads only session records, synced sessions and `GoalService`; the work-hours guard's unlock is `_workHours.Goal = _deepWork.UnlockProgress`, Budget days and the plan read `FocusedOn`, the overlay's goal line `Progress`; `WorkHoursGuard`, `UnlockService`, `LightsOutService`, `PreWorkService` and `WorkGateService` reference nothing in `Services.Goals` or `Core.Goals`; the phone's focusDay goal is the `goal` minutes the PC writes (`SyncCoordinator.GoalMinutes`). ### R6b2 review fixes

- **Trusted clock (blocker):** `CompanionApp` reads `TrustedTime.now()` once per environment read and passes it to `GuardsLock.at` and (as an offset time) to the weekly rule; `PhoneDeepWorkWeekly`'s default environment also uses `TrustedTime`. No system clock in the wait or session path. Remaining `now()` in the R6b2 diff are edit stamps and display (what-it-takes, target line, overview), none decides a wait or a session.
- **Pending drop:** `GoalEditorSheets.save` calls `set()` when the typed weekly target equals the current one and a lowering waits (the rule answers Unchanged and drops it). Caveat: the draft starts at the current target, so saving any other edit of Deep work while a lowering waits also drops it (same as the PC's rule path); Cancel stays the explicit way. Say if you want it limited to an edited field.
- **Backup:** Deep work's weekly target is restored only when the goals store is Ready and its import was not refused.
- **Areas lock:** Options > Areas (`GoalAreasSheet`) follows `LocalGuardsLock` like Settings > Goals > Areas: rest note, Add, reorder and Edit disabled. Not locked: Edit area opened by holding an area on the Goals screen, and "+ Area" inside the goal editor (outside the ask; flagged).
- **Commit / backup:** `PhoneDeepWorkWeekly.onChanged` (wired to auto backup); `PhoneGoalStore.updateAreas` saves a reorder in one commit.
- **Checks:** Kotlin 696/696 (was 693: skipped non-Deep-work / out-of-range fixture cases are no longer counted, 4 new wiring checks: passed-instant-only wait, early vs due settle, session holds, typing back drops the wait).

### BM1 done (Beeminder datapoints count toward goals; ADR 84)
- **What:** a goal with a Beeminder source (direction Read, switched on, a goal picked) now counts that Beeminder goal's datapoints on the PC. `CalendarNetwork.GetBeeminderDatapointsAsync` (one GET `/users/me/goals/<slug>/datapoints.json?sort=daystamp&count=300|100&auth_token=`; slug `[A-Za-z0-9_-]+` checked and escaped, else `ArgumentException` before any request) is called by `BeeminderDatapointsService` after each goals read (BeeminderService's new `GoalsRead` hook: 30-minute timer, Today or Goals opening via `RefreshIfStale`, token save, Refresh/Test) and when a goal is newly linked (a goals read, at most one a minute). First read keeps about 90 days; later reads only days from the newest stored day. Parser keeps day, value, id (+ timestamp for first/last ordering only, never stored); comments dropped. `BeeminderDayValue` applies goals.json `aggday` (sum, last, first, min, max, count, mean/truemean, uniqmean, median; else sum). `BeeminderEntries`: stable id per goal + slug + day (SHA-256 of the three), update in place, a deleted datapoint inside the window removes its day, a tombstoned entry stays gone; `Prune` removes Beeminder entries no live Read link owns (unlink, direction Send/Both, source off, goal deleted, token removed: runs on every store change and on `BeeminderService.Changed`). Entries go through `GoalStore.ChangeAsync` (sealed `goals.entries`). Hand wins by the existing `GoalCounting` rule (unchanged).
- **Goal slug:** `BeeminderGoal` now has `Slug` and `AggDay` (defaults empty, so the stored answer still loads); the source stores the real slug (the editor's picker key was the title before; a rule that stored a title is matched by title and saved as the slug on the next Save).
- **UI:** the Beeminder row says "Counts its daily values on this PC · <needed>", then "Read 2:05 PM" (12-hour) or, after a failed read, "Couldn't reach Beeminder · keeps the last values" (read when the dialog opens, not live); the tile text no longer says "Read one of your goals from the list this PC keeps". Send/Both stay "coming soon" pills (ADR 80). Day panel already said "from Beeminder". Phone unchanged.
- **Not done on purpose:** no sending; Beeminder entries are NOT in the sync file or guards/unlock/focusDay/Stats (checked); they ARE in a goals backup like other entries (see Q562).
- **Checks:** AppChecks 564 (536 + 28 `bm1/...`; two runs of 564 after the final build), GoalsChecks 247, Core 642, AccountSettingsChecks 68, CalendarExportChecks 123, solution build 0 errors. Mutation: `BeeminderDayValue` ignoring aggday failed `bm1/aggday` and `bm1/entries`, restored. Render harness (`HARNESS_DIALOG_SIZE`, `HARNESS_BEEMINDER_FAIL` env added to the temp harness): `%TEMP%/claude/bm1-shots/goal-edit-940x740.png`, `goal-edit-901x567-fail.png`; debug app 20 s on a temp data folder, alive, no stderr, no token so no call. Logs `%TEMP%/claude/tasks-ui-logs/bm1-*.log`.
- **Flake seen once:** one AppChecks run had 5 failures (4 `m5t4` and one `bm1/token`, my throttle test before I advanced the clock); two later runs and the final one passed 564/564. `m5t4` is not touched by BM1 (looks timing-dependent under load).

### BM1 review fixes
- Re-read window: each read starts at min(newest stored day, today - 14 days) (first read: today - 90, never earlier), so back-filled or edited days land. Paging: `count` page first, then `page=2..5&per=count` (at most 5 calls per goal and round) until the window is covered or a page is short; at the cap the oldest (possibly cut) day is dropped and the window starts the day after it. `Apply` deletes only inside the covered window.
- Goal matching: `BeeminderDatapointsService.FindGoal` = slug first, title only when exactly one goal has it; a shared title skips the link; the source row says "Pick the Beeminder goal again" (with the failure line, not instead of it). Round failures: network, 429 and 5xx stop the round, another 4xx / unreadable answer skips that goal (flags Failed) and the rest are read.
- Nits: aggday mean (unique), truemean, uniqmean, binary, nonzero, cap1 (code comment says reasoning only, from beebrain); slug regex `\z`; kick for a new link waits 5 min between kicks and retries (10 s, 6 times) when a read is under way; editor shows "Read 2:05 PM" and the failure line together; hard-rules BM1 bullet updated (backup exception, window, paging).
- Checks: AppChecks 578/578 (564 + 14 `bm1fix/...`; `bm1/entries` check rewritten as `bm1fix/window`), GoalsChecks 247, Core 661 (ST1's added), solution build 0 errors. Logs `tasks-ui-logs/bm1fix-*.log`. The kick-retry check really waits about 10 s.
- Files: Services/Beeminder/{BeeminderDatapointsService,BeeminderDayValue,BeeminderEntries}.cs, Services/CalendarNetwork.cs (optional `page`), ViewModels/GoalSourceRowViewModel.cs, App.axaml.cs, doc/hard-rules.md.

### GS0 done (forward-compatible goal JSON)

- **Rule (ADR 79 forward compatibility, both platforms, parity-checked):** a goal list written by a newer app never makes the store Unreadable and is never stripped. Unknown fields on every object (area, goal, target, waiting change, second target, source rule, app entry, pause, extra tiles, entry, reminder, review, decision) are kept and written back after the known fields. An item holding an enum value this version doesn't know (name or number) is kept as written at its place in its list (stored lists, a goal's sources, a review's decisions), never counted or shown; only damaged JSON still closes the goals. No migration (`goals.model` stays 1); known-only lists write byte for byte as before.
- **C# Core (new):** `GoalEnumConverter<T>` (replaces `JsonStringEnumConverter` on the 11 goal-item enums; names any case, defined numbers, number text; unknown -> `GoalNewerValueException : JsonException`), `GoalUnknownFields` (`[JsonExtensionData]` `Extra` on 12 records, value equality so records still compare), `GoalNewerItem`, `GoalNewerList<T>` + `GoalNewerListConverter<T>` (on `Goal.Sources`, `GoalReviewRecord.Decisions`), `GoalNewerJson` (`ReadList` / `WriteList` / `NewerOf`), `GoalUnknowns` (edits keep unknowns), `GoalAreaTidy` (area rule).
- **C# App:** `GoalLists.Newer` (`GoalNewerLists`); `GoalStore` reads / writes through `GoalNewerJson`, `Normalize` uses `GoalAreaTidy` and ends with `GoalUnknowns` (unknowns and newer items from before a change come back); `GoalsExport` lists read leniently (a newer backup item is skipped, the rest restores).
- **Kotlin shared:** `extra: JsonObject` on the 12 data classes, `Goal.newerSources`, `GoalReviewRecord.newerDecisions`, `GoalLists.newer` (`GoalNewerLists`), `GoalNewerItem`, `GoalNewerValueException`, `GoalJson.readItems` / `writeItems` / `readStoredLists` (unknown names kept, the PC's ignored names dropped), `GoalUnknowns.keep`, `GoalAreaTidy`; `GoalListRules.tidy(lists, now, before)` = the PC's `Normalize` (Q575); `imported` keeps the device's newer items. **App:** `PhoneGoalStore` reads with `readStoredLists`, writes newer items back, tidies with `before`; reminder "unchanged" test ignores unknown fields.
- **Decisions:** Q575 (tidy rule from C#, deterministic stamps, phone no longer "first live area"), Q576-Q580 (unit kept as written, store-level keep, tombstones, backups, dropped names). `doc/hard-rules.md`: one GS0 sentence in each goals bullet (PC, phone). No sync parts, no UI, no new dependency or stored key.
- **Checks:** Core 699/699 (661 + 38 `gs0/...`: fields at every level, unknown enum per kind incl. numbers, order, bytes, damaged, edits, equality, tidy), AppChecks 584/584 (578 + 6 `gs0/store` + `gs0/backup`: newer store stays Ready, rebuilt goal and `new GoalLists` keep everything, reopen, newer backup), GoalsChecks 247/247; fixtures `gs0/round-trip` (29), `gs0/keep` (13), `gs0/tidy` (9) from `Gs0Fixtures.cs` (out-of-repo checks, backups `*.gs0bak`); Kotlin `:checks:run --offline` 1115/1115 (1056 + 51 cases + 3 presence + `gs0` fully checked + 4 `goals-store` GS0 self checks), byte-identical text vs C# on every round-trip / keep case. Mutations: C# entry `[JsonExtensionData]` removed -> 1 fail; C# newer item dropped -> 13 fail; Kotlin `putExtra` no-op -> 17 fail; Kotlin newer item dropped -> 14 fail; all restored. Solution build 0 errors; `assembleDebug assembleRelease --offline` green (R8; no reflection); gradle stopped. Logs `tasks-ui-logs/gs0-*.log`.

### NAV2 done (PC Back/Forward like a browser)

- **What:** title-bar buttons are ‹ › (`ChevronLeft/Right`, same 18 px icon in the same 28 x 32 slot; tooltips "Back · Alt+←" / "Forward · Alt+→"). Alt+← / Alt+→ navigate everywhere except a text box (`NavigationFocus.ColumnHeader` and `TaskColumnView.IsHeader` removed); the Tasks column move is now Ctrl+Alt+← / Ctrl+Alt+→ (header tooltips, accessible name, Settings' keyboard map (two new rows), comments, hard-rules). Two-finger swipe: new `Services/Navigation/SwipeTracker` (pure; horizontal `PointerWheelChanged`, Delta.X dominant, summed, 250 ms idle reset, threshold 3 notches, one nav per swipe, inertia ignored), `SwipeState`, `SwipePhase`, `HorizontalScrollGuard` (any ancestor `ScrollViewer` that can scroll sideways at all, also at its end, turns the swipe off for that whole swipe), `SwipeBubblePlan` (44 px bubble: offset -44 to 12, opacity = progress, accent fill at the threshold, greyed with nowhere to go). The bubble is `SwipeLayer` / `SwipeBubble` in `MainWindow.axaml` over the page area (clipped to it, not hit-testable, theme tokens only, shown full for 160 ms on commit). The swipe is off while a dialog, the unsaved-settings prompt or the sync popover is open. Page slide: `HistorySlidePlan` (48 px, 140 ms ease-in out, 220 ms ease-out in; back mirrored), `HistorySlideRun` (frame timer, render transform + opacity only, `FinishNow()`), `Motion.HistorySlide`, `MainWindowViewModel.HistoryNavigating` (+ `HistoryNavigatingEventArgs`, `FinishHistoryMove`), handled in `MainWindow` (tab change: out, apply, in, the tab's own Enter skipped; same-tab place: out, apply, in; Animations Off = event not handled, applied at once, no transform; a rail click during a slide finishes it first; tab clicks keep today's Enter).
- **Owner addition (swipe direction setting):** Settings › On screen & sound (the Alerts & on-screen page, next to Animations) has a new group "Touchpad swipe" with "Swipe direction": Default (swipe right = Back, like Chrome on Windows) / Natural (opposite, like a Mac), hint "... Natural: swipe left to go back, like a Mac." Stored as `MotionSettings.Swipe` (`SwipeStyle`) in the existing device-local `motion` document (already in `DeviceSettingsRegistry.DeviceKeys`, so it is in the PC backup's device group; an older document reads Default), applies at once (read per wheel event; no wait, no lock; not a guard setting), never synced or in a profile. `SwipeRightMeans` is gone; `SwipeTracker.PositiveDeltaXIsSwipeRight` (the Delta.X sign) and `SwipeTracker.Style` replace it.
- **Deviations from the brief:** the wheel handler is registered with `handledEventsToo: true` (a page that scrolls up and down takes the swipe's small vertical part and marks the event handled, which would silence most swipes; sideways scrollers are excluded by the guard instead). The slide is a 16 ms frame timer on opacity / `TranslateTransform.X`, not Avalonia `Animation` (fill mode and cancellation end values were not predictable). Focus restore after the slide: nothing extra was needed (the same-tab slide never moves focus; a tab change loses the focused control exactly as before). The bubble hides without easing at every speed. The plan doc's NAV2 entry is not written (outside my files).
- **Docs:** `doc/hard-rules.md` UI "Back / forward (PC)" (chevrons, Alt+arrows, swipe + scroll rule + setting, tilt-wheel note, slide) and the Column order bullet (Ctrl+Alt+arrows).
- **Checks:** AppChecks **613/613** (584 + 29 `nav2/...`: tracker progress / threshold / direction / vertical ignored / pause / one-per-swipe / reverse, swipe-style Default and Natural, the setting stored and reloaded and an old document reads Default, Settings row text and place, guard wiring + pure rule, keys (Alt vs Ctrl+Alt), column tooltips, Settings map, chevrons and tooltips, slide numbers, Animations Off = no transform and applied once, speeds, wiring, bubble numbers and XAML, docs; 6 old `tbfix/...` and 5 `r3/...` names or expectations updated for the column header and the chevrons); GoalsChecks 247/247; Core 699/699; AccountSettingsChecks 68/68; CalendarExportChecks 123/123; solution 0 errors (`tasks-ui-logs/nav2-build.log`); Debug app ran 22 s on `round2-data/nav2-devstart-*`, no error output. Render harness (new `Nav2Check.cs`, view `swipe-bubble`): 13/13 `nav2/...` (guard over real ScrollViewers incl. at the end and nested, `HistorySlideRun` mid-way / FinishNow / full run, bubble inside the page area), fit 30/30 at 1440 x 880 and 901 x 567 (the first 901 run showed 1 transient Today/Instrument-light fit failure that did not repeat in 3 further runs), menus 10/10, navigation 9/9, every `tbfix/` line passes at both sizes, `BackButton` / `ForwardButton` anchors unchanged against `round2-shots-tbfix2(-901)`. Known baseline moves, not NAV2: `NoiseControl:left` 155 to 203 at 901 (the narrower Start pill, 494e890d), rail `auto:Goals` 318 to 251 (a rail change since that baseline), GoalsCheck r5a lines at 901 (written for 1440, Q492). Shots: `%TEMP%/claude/round2-shots-nav2` (1440 x 880) and `round2-shots-nav2-901`; logs `tasks-ui-logs/nav2-*.log`. Out-of-repo backups `*.nav2bak`.
- **Hand test for the owner (touchpad; a swipe can't be simulated):** (1) Two fingers right on the touchpad: Back with the slide and the bubble from the left edge; left: Forward. With Settings › Touchpad swipe set to Natural both flip. If Default is the wrong way round on your touchpad, flip `SwipeTracker.PositiveDeltaXIsSwipeRight` (one constant). (2) Too sensitive or too stiff: `SwipeTracker.ThresholdNotches` (3.0). (3) Tasks columns when they scroll sideways (narrow window, Group by Label/Tag): the swipe scrolls them and never navigates, also at their ends; with the Shelf columns fitting, it navigates. (4) A swipe over a page that scrolls only up and down (Settings, Stats) navigates. (5) Mouse back / forward buttons, Alt+← / Alt+→ (also with a column header focused), the chevrons and the swipe all slide; Ctrl+Alt+← / → moves a column; Alt+← / → in the add box still moves its split. (6) Settings › Animations Off: no slide anywhere, the swipe still navigates; Fast / Slow scale the slide. (7) Press Back twice quickly: the first slide ends at once, the second runs. (8) A dialog, the Save / Discard prompt or the sync popover open: the swipe does nothing. (9) A tilt-wheel mouse also navigates (accepted, as in Chrome).

### R7.1 done (Kotlin task model + field merge)

- **What it is:** the PC's ADR 76 task model and merges in Kotlin, pure (no Android API, no serialization plugin, R8-safe), parity-checked against the PC on the PC's own JSON. **Nothing on the phone stores, sends or shows it yet** (R7.2, R7.3); the phone's old stores are untouched. One live change: the phone now keeps and passes on a `later` item's `bin` marker (Q10; `SyncLaterItem.bin`, `SyncFileJson.writeLater` / `readLater`; the tie-break follows the PC's).
- **Package `android/shared/.../shared/tasks/`** (one type per file): `TaskItem` (every PC field, also the ones the phone has no screen for: calendar, plan, conflicts, numbers, devices, effort, Auto-saved day), `TaskStep`, `TaskReminder` + `TaskReminderOccurrence`, `TaskConflict`, `TaskNumber`, `TaskBin` / `TaskHistoryKind` / `TaskReminderStatus` (`wire` = the file's name), `TaskLabel` (+ `TaskLabelColors`, `TaskLabelBook`), `TaskView` / `TaskViewConfig` / `TaskViewFilter`, `TaskHistoryEntry`, the sync wrappers `SyncTaskItem` / `SyncTaskLabel` / `SyncTaskView` / `SyncTaskLogItem` (`SharedItem`s; `tieBreak` = the PC's JSON text), `SyncTaskAccountSettings`, `TaskField` + `TaskFieldStamps`, `TaskParts` / `TaskRead` / `TaskLists`.
- **JSON: `TaskJson`** (by hand): `readParts` / `writeParts` for `tasks`, `labels`, `views`, `taskLog`, `taskAccountSettings`; lenient and cleaned exactly like the PC's lenient converters + `SyncFileCodec.WithCleanTaskParts`; `text()` gives the PC's bytes (through `GoalJson.text`). Times are `OffsetDateTime` cut to .NET ticks with **the offset kept** (`DotNetTime`), ids lower case. The old Kotlin types still keep `Instant`s (offsets lost): only the new model round-trips byte for byte.
- **Beyond the PC (which drops both):** every model class has `extra` (unknown fields, written back after the known ones; `TaskUnknown`), and an entry with an enum value this version doesn't know is handed back as written in `TaskRead.newer` / `TaskParts.newer...` (never merged or shown). `unknown = false` on the writers gives the PC's own shape (used for tie-breaks and canonical values). A merge keeps the unknown fields of the copy edited last (then the later text), so it stays order-independent.
- **Merges:** `TaskFieldMerge` (stamps, bases, "Changed on both devices", Q173 `winnerFirstBases`, numbers, the clamp, whole-field reminders), `TaskEdits` (`edit`, `resolve`), `TaskLabelMerge` (rings on by its own stamp, tombstones kept for good: `SharedListMerge.merge(..., keepTombstones)` is new), `TaskAccountSettingsMerge` (+ `clean`), `TaskListMerge` (`mergeLists` = the PC's `TaskListRules.MergeLists`; views and History item by item), `TaskRingDevices`, `TaskNumbering` (`numberingDevice`: lowest PC, else lowest phone; `assignFinal`), `TaskFieldValues` (canonical texts, hash).
- **Shared stamp helper for GS2 (orchestrator review item 1):** `sync/FieldStamp` (at, by, bases, extra), `sync/FieldStamps` (`firstIsNewer`, `firstWins` for a value that may be unstamped, `next` = the next-stamp rule, `clamped`), `sync/DotNetTime` (ticks, cut, same moment, MIN, epoch). Tasks, labels and account settings all go through it; goals should too.
- **Old fields:** `OldTaskFieldsWriter` (twin of the PC's: the old lists rebuilt from the task list, `bin: "next"`, bin spacing, tombstones for moved tasks, Auto-saved never twice, History's finished ones ticked) + `OldTaskFields` / `OldTaskList`. **Not built: the `OldTaskFieldsReader` twin** (a file without `tasks` read into the new model; Q583), and `TaskListRules.Normalize` / `TaskHistoryRules` (store rules): R7.2.
- **Migration rule:** `TaskMigration.run(TaskMigrationInput)` over the phone's own old types (`SyncNextTask`, `SyncLaterItem`, `SyncParked`, `SyncAutoSavedItem`, `SyncCategory`, `TaskMigrationPreset` = the phone's saved filter) -> `TaskMigrationResult` with `TaskMigrationCounts` (`isBalanced`). Same rule as the PC's; times come out in UTC (the old stores keep moments). R7.2 runs it behind a backup + marker and keeps the old stores.
- **Known differences from the PC, on purpose (all on input the PC never writes):** a step without a title or a reminder without a rule leaves its task out (the PC keeps `null`); a tombstone with a `null` title has `""`; an enum given as a wrong-case name is set aside as a newer app's (the PC skips the entry); a planned day part's unknown fields are not kept; a text that is exactly a .NET time is written unescaped (Q424, inherited from `GoalJson.text`).
- **Found in the C# merge while writing fixtures (not changed; PC lane, Q584-Q586):** (1) merging a result again with one of its inputs can reorder a title's bases (same stamp, same value, different bases: the first argument's go first), so two devices can hold different base orders; (2) `MergeItem` turns "no stamps" into explicit creation-time stamps, so a list's text changes the first time two copies of an unstamped task meet; (3) `TaskFieldMerge.Folded` calls `.Single()` on a merge that drops tombstones older than 30 days: a list holding the same id twice with an old tombstone winning would throw (the Kotlin twin keeps the winner and lets the outer merge drop it). The Kotlin twin mirrors (1) and (2) exactly.
- **Checks:** C# Core 699/699 (unchanged; it now also writes `r71/*`: `tasks-ui-checks/R71Fixtures.cs`, hooked into `Round2Fixtures.WriteAll`, backups `*.r71bak`; it prints `NOTE` lines for (1) and (2)). Kotlin `:checks:run --offline` **1525/1525** (1115 + 410: 371 cases in 12 files + 12 presence + 1 "every r71 file has a check" + 26 `r71-self`). Files: task-json 41 (BINDING PC -> phone -> PC round trip of a task with every field, byte for byte, also as a History snapshot), values 9, field-merge 174 (58 scenarios x forward / swapped / again), task-edits 19, label-merge 48, account-settings 33, lists-merge 15, ring-devices 14, numbering 5, old-fields-write 6, old-fields-json 2, migration 5. `checks/build.gradle.kts` now compiles every pure file of `:shared`'s root, `sync`, `goals` and `tasks` packages (`sharedExcluded` = the 7 that need Android or `SyncKey`), so `SyncFileJson` is checkable. Old fields are compared by what the PC reads (`OldFieldJson`: the phone has always written `1.0`, UTC times, an empty `subtasks` in each step and no `isDone`).
- **Mutations (each failed, then restored):** reminders merged per occurrence -> 2 fail; Q173 union dropped -> 4; `calendar` not written back -> 24; label tombstones aged out -> 3. `assembleDebug assembleRelease --offline` green; gradle stopped. Logs `tasks-ui-logs/r71-*.log`. Carry list: `round2-deliverables/r71-carry.md` (8 done as model or rule with their wiring named, 9 wait with reason and task). `doc/hard-rules.md`: one R7.1 sentence at the end of the ADR 76 bullet.

### GS1a done (goals sync parts and field merge, C#)

- **Scope:** C# Core only (ADR 79 Accepted; plan check §3 "Merge core", orchestrator review items 1, 5 and GS0 carries a-d). No App wiring (GS3), no Kotlin (GS2), no new dependency, no stored key, no schema/profile change.
- **Sync file:** five optional parts `goals`, `goalAreas`, `goalEntries`, `goalReminders`, `goalReviews` (null-omitted; `SyncGoalListConverter<T>`: non-array = none, damaged item skipped, an item with an unknown enum kept raw at its place and written back). `SyncFileBuilder.ForQr` strips them. `SyncGoalParts.Into` / `From` (no file parts at all -> null = keep own goals; waiting changes stripped both ways; tombstones cleaned).
- **Stamps:** `GoalField` (Name, Look, Area, Target = kind+target, SecondTarget (+ Deep work's Track time only), Sources, Pauses, ShowOnToday, ExtraTiles, Notes), `GoalFieldStamp(At, By)` (no bases), `GoalFieldStamps` (JSON `stamps` on `Goal`, optional; missing = every part at `EditedAt` by the empty id; unknown stamp names kept). `GoalFieldValues` (part value = the goal's own JSON names/raw values, so Kotlin can match byte for byte), `GoalEdits.New / Edit / Restamp` (the first edit of an unstamped goal gives its other parts the OLD edit time).
- **Model:** `ISharedItem` on `Goal`, `GoalArea`, `GoalEntry`, `GoalReminder`, `GoalReviewRecord` (`TieBreak` = SyncJson incl. unknown fields, `[JsonIgnore]`). `Goal.TrackTimeOnly` (JSON `trackTimeOnly`, only when true; travels on Deep work's record only, never stored). Known-only JSON unchanged byte for byte (both new properties omitted when unset).
- **Merge** `GoalSyncMerge.Merge(first, second, now)` (pure, symmetric, idempotent): goals part by part on live copies (stamp time, device id, value), then `SharedListMerge` (tombstones, revive, order, 30 days, 2-min clamp, applied to part stamps too); areas / entries / reminders whole item; reviews re-keyed `GoalReviewIds.For(week)` (v5 id, `TaskLabel.IdFor` scheme, new namespace), same-week records fold (base fields from the newest, each goal's decision from the newest record, content breaks a same-time tie); raw newer items merge by id + stamps (`GoalRawItem`), unreadable ones carried along. Pristine seeds (`GoalSeeds` = Core copy of the first run's Work / Deep work; an AppCheck pins it equal to `GoalDefaults`): a live untouched Work is dropped when another copy is edited; a Deep work part still at the seed value with the empty-id stamp loses to any other. Deep work: tombstones ignored, kind / target / source / no pauses forced, `BuiltIn` only on it; created = the earlier (all goals). Tidy: `GoalAreaTidy.Rehome` after the merge, plus the moved goal's Area part stamped (EditedAt, empty id) so the move holds. Delete rule: `GoalTombstones.Delete` no longer touches entries / reminders; `IsShown` hides them while the goal isn't live; the merge drops them once the goal is gone from the lists (tombstone pruned). Tombstones: no text, no unknown fields, no stamps (carry a). Unknown fields: kept from the other copy only where the winner has none (carry b); a newer writer's own list wins whole.
- **Left for GS3 (App):** `GoalsService.DeleteGoalAsync` -> `GoalTombstones.Delete` (stop tombstoning entries / reminders) and hide via `IsShown`; every goal change through `GoalEdits.Restamp` (one place: `GoalStore.ChangeAsync`); `GoalStore.Normalize`'s rehome must stamp the Area part like the merge does (else the next merge can flip it back and the tidy stamp creeps); Deep work: `GoalDeepWorkSync.Compose` with the EFFECTIVE weekly / Track time only and their OWN stamp (kept apart from the record's), `Split` hands the received ones to the lowering rule; `GoalDefaults` may delegate to `GoalSeeds`; store-level waiting changes re-attached after a merge (the merge leaves them out); review service: new records with `GoalReviewIds.For(week)`.
- **Checks:** Core 814/814 (699 + 115 `gs1a/...`: 30 merge cases x holds / both ways / again (merged result read back from JSON and merged with each input and itself), field, stamps, edits, seeds, Deep work compose / split, review id, delete / hidden, file parts (names, QR, pending, partial, seal+open, lenient, older file), order, size). AppChecks 628/628 (`r4t2/sync` rewritten: goal types reach `SyncFile` only through the five parts; + `gs1a/seeds` parity). GoalsChecks 247/247. Solution build 0 errors. Fixtures `round2-fixtures/gs1a/`: merge (30 cases), review-id (4), deep-work (3), edits (5), tombstones (1), parts (1) from `tasks-ui-checks/Gs1aFixtures.cs` (+ `Gs1aParts.cs`, `Gs1aCase.cs`, `Gs1aChecks.cs`; backups `*.gs1abak`). Mutations: whole-goal newest wins -> 8 fail; pristine rule off -> 4 fail; both restored. Logs `tasks-ui-logs/gs1a-*.log`.
- **Size fixture (owner: all history travels; measured only):** 3 years x 20 goals x 3 devices = 65,700 entries: JSON 12.65 MB, sealed 1.86 MB, serialize 155 ms, compress 235 ms, seal + open 702 ms, merge 655 ms. Below the 2 MB sealed line, but see Q591 (the 16 MB open limit).

### R7.2 done (phone writes the task parts)

- **What it is:** the phone keeps and syncs the one task list (ADR 76). Its old Tasks screens still run (R7.3 replaces them) on views of it. The old stores are never written or deleted after the switch (the way back); the phone keeps writing the old fields for one release.
- **Store** `app/.../tasks/PhoneTaskStore.kt`: prefs `task_model` (`lists` = the five parts + a newer app's entries, `viewSearches`), Keystore-sealed, every commit normalized (`TaskListRules.normalize`: settle into History, numbers, prune, reconcile). **Migration** `PhoneTaskModelMigration.kt` (in `CompanionApp.onCreate`, after sync is set up): sealed copies of the old stores first (`before-model-<version>`, once per version; + the folder backups when a folder is chosen) -> `TaskMigration` -> `TaskModelSwitch.decide` (refused unless balanced) -> lists + marker in one `commit()`; counts-only log `files/companion/tasks-model.log`.
- **Shared, pure (`shared/tasks/`):** `TaskListRules` (normalize, settled, numbered, pruned, endOf, `mergeFile` = the Q583 rule, `fileParts`), `TaskHistoryRules` (list, remove, doAgain, bringBack, reconciled), `TaskFacadeLists` (old lists as views), `TaskLocalEdits` (old-list changes -> `TaskEdits`; `move` keeps the task), `TaskLabelEdits`, `TaskModelSwitch`, `TaskNewerEntries` (review item 3); `sync/SyncFileStrip` (kept copy, QR); `SyncFile.taskParts` + `SyncFileJson` read/write.
- **Wiring:** `SharedListsStore.attach` (views; edits via `TaskLocalEdits`; moves via `move`; old-field and Auto-saved merges off; handovers into the list; used-ids rule), `TaskCategoriesStore.attach` (labels; own list no longer written), `SyncCoordinator` (`taskParts` out under "Sync task lists", `onTaskParts` in, `SyncFileStrip` for the kept copy and QR), `PhoneKindBackup` (task-lists kind carries `tasks`/`labels`/`views`/`taskLog`/`accountSettings` + old lists; restore through the merge).
- **Q583 decision (orchestrator):** no `OldTaskFieldsReader` twin; a received file without `tasks` leaves the task list untouched (account settings still merge; a QR file carries no lists, as before); written into `doc/hard-rules.md` (ADR 76 bullet, the phone part rewritten for R7.2). Q60 stays as logged (the PC sends no mode field: needs ADR 42). Questions Q597-Q606.
- **R7.1 review fixes (same commit):** `SharedListMerge.compare` returns 0 for the same/equal item; `TaskFieldMerge` builds a field's text only on a time+device tie (5k tasks, both sides edited every task: ~160 ms here vs the review's 430-470 ms; 2k ~100 ms; `TIMING` lines in the Kotlin checks log); `now` cut to ticks in `TaskFieldMerge.merge` and `TaskEdits`; `folded` keys by lower-case id; Q584 full-tie rule on the Kotlin side (bases joined with ",", ordinal; Q598: the PC lane must match; no fixture case had to be marked pending); `GoalJson` writes an unknown number back unchanged (`keepsUnknown` compares values now; a self-check with 1.0 / 1.50 / a long decimal / 1e400); `TaskModelSelfCheck` compares texts, its self-merge check replaced by a real PC-edit merge; newer entries replaced by id per file (`TaskNewerEntries`, self-checked).

Kotlin `:checks:run --offline` **1590/1590** (1528 after the review fixes + 62: 28 `r72` cases (normalize 7, history 6, store-migration 3 on a realistic store, old-fields-session 2 for Q128, receive-without-tasks 5 for Q583, strip 1, with-parts 4) + 7 presence + 1 every-file + 26 `r72-self`). Logs `tasks-ui-logs/r72-*.log`. Carry list rows 1, 5-9, 16 updated.

(2) `files/companion/tasks-model.log` says `balanced=true` and `switched over`. (3) With "Sync task lists" on, edit a task's title on the phone and sync: the PC shows the edit and the task keeps its number; tick a Later item on the PC: the phone shows it ticked. (4) Move a Later item to Today on the phone: on the PC it is the same task (same number) in This session or Queued. (5) Delete a category on the phone: its tasks show No label on both. **Added by the R7.2 review (do these before relying on a phone build):** (6) `adb logcat -s TaskModel` while the app starts, and `adb shell run-as com.accountabilibuddy.companion cat files/companion/tasks-model.log`: counts and states only, no task text; after the switch a restart says `task list read`. (7) Delete a task on the phone, tap Undo: it comes back with the same number (the ID chip). (8) Turn Sync task lists off, edit a task on the PC and on the phone, turn it back on and sync: both edits arrive, nothing is deleted. (9) Tick a task late in the evening and untick it the next morning (or tick it, wait past midnight, untick): it comes back to its list with its number, and History drops it. (10) Restore the `before-model` / pre-update task-lists backup (the Task lists backup card › Restore): the tasks come back over later edits and nothing is duplicated. (11) During a PC session, Add to Today a Later task: the PC's checklist gets it, the phone takes it out of Later; edit its title on the phone before the PC answers: it stays in Later too, with the message.

### GS1a review fixes (+ Q584, Q586)

- **Scope:** C# Core only (review of `ed8753c0`); no App change, no Kotlin, no dependency, no schema change. Files: `Goals/GoalSyncMerge.cs`, `Goals/GoalSyncClean.cs` (new), `Goals/GoalAreaTidy.cs`, `Goals/GoalEdits.cs`, `Goals/GoalFieldValues.cs`, `Goals/GoalTombstones.cs`, `Goals/GoalReviewDecision.cs`, `Goals/GoalDeepWorkSync.cs`, `Sync/SyncFileCodec.cs`, `Sync/SyncGoalParts.cs`, `Sync/TaskFieldMerge.cs`.
- **1 (blocker) tidy stamp creep:** the merge's tidy move stamps the goal's Area part `(new edit time, GoalAreaTidy.StampId)`, `StampId = d33b0a11-6f0c-4e2a-9d51-0000000000b1` (never the empty id, which read as the untouched Deep work seed). `GoalAreaTidy.Rehome` itself is unchanged (GS0 parity); **GS3 `GoalStore.Normalize` and GS2's merge must stamp a moved goal's Area part the same way, with the same id.**
- **2 (blocker) incomplete items:** `GoalSyncClean` (Areas / Goals / Entries / Reminders / Reviews / Lists) runs in `SyncFileCodec.Open` (`WithCleanGoalParts`), `SyncGoalParts.Into/From` and on both merge inputs. Rules (Kotlin GS2 mirrors): empty id dropped (newer raw items too); live area without name dropped; live goal without name, target or sources dropped (also when it carries unknown fields: a known-kind goal lacking them is damaged; an unknown-kind goal is raw before this rule); missing icon/colour = ""; entry/reminder without goal id dropped; review without decisions = []; a tombstone needs only id + stamps: `GoalTombstones.Clean` fills a missing target with `PlaceholderTarget` = `{"period":"Day","unit":"Count","direction":"AtLeast"}` (Kotlin must also accept a tombstone without areaId/kind/createdAt: written back as the empty id, `Habit`, `0001-01-01T00:00:00+00:00`). `WithoutPending`, review folding null-safe.
- **3 no-op Restamp:** no part changed -> `Stamps = before.Stamps ?? GoalFieldStamps.For(before)`, `EditedAt = before.EditedAt`.
- **4 waiting change:** `GoalFieldValues.Canonical/All` read the goal without `pendingChange` (so Edit/Restamp/merge never stamp a waiting change; the Target part is stamped when the change lands through Restamp).
- **5 raw Deep work:** a newer app's Deep work tombstone is dropped (`GoalSyncClean.Goals`); documented: a newer app's live copy wins whole when newer.
- **6 reviews:** `GoalReviewDecision.DecidedAt` (JSON `decidedAt`, optional, omitted when null). Fold: each goal's decision = newest by `decidedAt ?? record.editedAt` (clamped to now + 2 min), tie -> ordinal JSON of the decision with its time written in; the winner is written WITH `decidedAt` (raw newer decisions get `"decidedAt"` added via JSON). Decisions ordered by goal id ("D" text, ordinal), then decisions without a readable goal id by their text. GS3: `GoalReviewService` should set `DecidedAt = now` when the user answers.
- **7 Split:** only `secondTarget` Week + Minutes + positive finite amount is a weekly target, else none. Doc: a removed/unreadable/lower weekly target and Track time only turning on are loosenings (rule application GS3/GS4).
- **NIT offsets (chosen: tie-break, not UTC on write):** the same instant with two offsets -> the smaller offset wins (stamps after time/device/value; created = earlier, edited = later). Kotlin: compare `OffsetDateTime.offset.totalSeconds`, smaller first.
- **Q592 implemented:** after the goal merge, a live pristine Work holding no live goal (known or raw `areaId`) is replaced by the newest Work tombstone of either input still within 30 days (clamped, cleaned); the tidy re-makes Work if a goal moves into it. Fixtures both ways.
- **Q586:** `TaskFieldMerge.Folded` passes `keepTombstones: true` (Kotlin twin's behaviour). `TaskStore.MergeFileAsync` runs inside `SyncCoordinator.AcceptGuardedAsync`, which catches everything but SQLite/cancel: a bad file is skipped as unreadable, never stops the loop (before the fix such a file was already stored by `_received.AcceptAsync` while its lists went unmerged). Check: `TaskListRules.MergeFile` with such a file doesn't throw.
- **Q584:** `TaskFieldMerge.PickText`: on a time + device + value tie, the copy whose bases joined with "," sort later (ordinal; none = ""; equal: first) is the newer, exactly the Kotlin rule (Q598). r71 field-merge gains 2 Q584 + 1 Q586 scenarios (each also swapped and again); the old Q173 "again changes it" NOTE is gone.
- **Checks:** Core 870/870 (814 + 56: 14 new gs1a merge cases x holds/both ways/again (fix 1-6, nit, q592) + 14 `gs1afix/...`). AppChecks 628/628 (Debug). GoalsChecks 247/247. Solution build 0 errors. Fixtures regenerated: `round2-fixtures/gs1a/` merge (44 cases), edits (+3: unstamped no-op, waiting alone, waiting landing), new `deep-work-split.json` (7), new `review-three.json` (a, b, c: every order equals expected), `r71/*`. Mutations: tidy stamp back to the empty id -> 5 fail; `GoalSyncClean` off -> 10 fail (fix 2 cases throw / fail, fix 5 Deep work deleted); both restored. Logs `tasks-ui-logs/gs1afix-*.log`. Harness: `Gs1aFixtures.cs`, `Gs1aChecks.cs` (merge cases now catch a throw as a FAIL), `R71Fixtures.cs` (backups `*.gs1afixbak`).

### R7.3a done (phone Tasks pages, strip, sheets)

- **What the owner sees on a phone:** Tasks is planning only. Header "Tasks" with Views, Options, Search (no gear) → the pinned **Today strip** ("This session 3 ready · Queued 2 · Reminders 1", "1/3" in a PC session; a tap opens Today; no timer) → the chip row (bins: dashed "+", All, a chip per label; by label / tag: "Grouped by label", All bins, To sort, Next, Later) → the Shelf **one page at a time**: a card per bin / label / tag with 24 dp peeks of the neighbours (grip lines, bin icon or label dot, the name vertical), dots under it or "4 / 9" past 5 pages. Pages change by a peek tap, a dot tap or a 40 dp swipe on the header or empty space, with a crossfade + 14 dp slide (170 ms × the Motion setting; Off = fade); a swipe that starts on a row is the row's (right → Today · Queued, left → Later / Next) and never changes pages. Rows: square tick, ★ + faint gold tint, the detail line with the label dot and the amber "Overdue 3 d", ⋮; a tap opens the task in place (steps with letters, note, reminders line, ID chip); holding the title 550 ms opens Rename. **Options** sheet (views chips; ARRANGE: Group the Shelf by, Page order, Sort incl. Shuffle, Rows, Pin highlights; SHOW ONLY: Due date, Labels, Tags, Time limit, Untouched for, Highlighted only; MORE: History, Tasks settings; footer Clear · Save · Show n tasks), **Page order** (Bins | Labels | Tags, drag ≡, start page radio, Wrap around), the **⋮ task sheet** (the PC row menu's rows in its order, with the Move to grid), and in a PC session the **hidden Shelf** card.
