### GS1b done (derived values, compact rows, month/year history files)
- **Scope:** C# Core only (ADR 79 Accepted; orchestrator review item 2 + "Shared history" + archive constraints; plan check §1 items 7-12). No App wiring (GS3), no transport, no Kotlin (GS2), no dependency, no schema/profile change. ADR 79: one sentence on the marker field.
- **Entries:** `GoalEntry.DeviceId` (JSON `deviceId`, only when set; null = hand / older entry), `GoalEntry.Stamp`; `GoalEntrySource.FocusLocal` (a PC's #tag extra; older apps keep it as a newer item). `GoalDerivedIds.For(goal, source, device, day)` = v5 SHA-1 over namespace `7c1d2e94-3b5a-4f60-9e8d-0a7b6c5d4e31` + UTF-8 `"<goal D>|<source enum name>|<device D>|yyyy-MM-dd"` (derived sources HealthConnect / ScreenTime / FocusLocal / Calendar; Beeminder keeps the PC's slug id).
- **Writer** `GoalDerivedWriter.Changes(held, device, readings, covers, now)`: own deterministic id only, updated in place only when the value changed (`NextStamp`), real 0 for Health Connect / screen time (FocusLocal / Calendar write 0 only over an earlier value), own day missing inside a `GoalDerivedCover` -> tombstone, own tombstone revived by a new reading; never hand, no-device, another device's or Beeminder entries. `MayChange` (Beeminder: only with a token). `Applied` puts changes in place.
- **Counting** (`GoalCounting`, `GoalSourceData.Steps` + `.DeviceId` added): per device first (Amount: first reading, Time: newest), then HC highest, screen time sum over `CountsOn` devices (no-device entries count), Beeminder one, FocusLocal = other devices' extras summed (self excluded), Calendar = max(0, others' highest - this device's own calendar extra); FocusLocal/Calendar only with a switched-on #tag / calendar rule, only Time + Habit, added to the time minutes and to `Sources`. Steps: a day with any live HC entry reads it, else `SyncSteps` (never both). `GoalDerivedMinutes.FocusLocalExtra(goal, ownSessions, day)` / `CalendarExtra(goal, data, day)` = what GS3 writes. Legacy single-device data counts exactly as before (GoalsChecks 247 unchanged).
- **Compact rows** (`GoalEntryRows`, `SyncGoalEntryListConverter`; now on `SyncFile.GoalEntries` and the history files): `{i,g,d,t,v,s,w,e,x,o,p}` in that order; ids base64url (RFC 4122 bytes, 22 chars); `d` = yyyyMMdd; `t` = TimeOnly ticks; `e`/`x` = **UTC .NET ticks** (64-bit int; Kotlin `(epochSecond*10^7 + nano/100) + 621355968000000000`); `o` = offset minutes (the phone writes local offsets, so the round trip stays exact); codes hc/st/bm/ca/fl, hand omitted; `i` omitted when the id is the derived one; defaults omitted. Object form when a row can't hold it exactly (unknown fields, two offsets). Reader: object with `g` = row; unknown code or row field = newer item kept as written; GS1a object form read too. `GoalRawItem` reads/re-stamps rows. A new source must be written in the object form (documented).
- **Window** `GoalSyncWindow.Recent/Apply(lists, now)`: entries with stamp >= now - 90 days (unreadable newer items kept). GS3 calls it before `SyncGoalParts.Into`.
- **Tombstone rule (chosen):** entry tombstones kept for good everywhere (`GoalSyncMerge` entries `keepTombstones: true`; history files; **GS3 `GoalStore` and GS2's merge must not prune entry tombstones**). A deleted goal's entries still go with its pruned tombstone.
- **History** (`GoalHistoryMonth`, `GoalHistoryYear`, `GoalHistoryPiece`, `GoalHistoryFileKey/Kind/Listing`, `GoalHistoryDevice`, `GoalHistoryRules`, `GoalHistoryCodec` "ABM1"/"ABY1" = the sync file's sealing; `SyncFileCodec.SealJson/OpenJson/SealedJsonLength` internal): month = UTC month of the stamp; a month file holds every entry the device holds in that month (own + received; hand entries carry no writer), by id; writable = current + previous UTC month; rewrite when changed, at most daily, plus one closing write after the month ends. `ToRead`: new/changed since last read (own skipped), all when new to the group, all of a device whose last complete merge is > 90 days (+ every year file). Roll-up from 1 Feb UTC by the active PC with the lowest id (D text, ordinal), adds only, lists pieces (device, month, version). `MayRemoveMonth`: frozen, piece version held, every active device's `goalYearsMerged` >= the year's `RolledAt`. `Marked` updates the marker. `Merge(held, file?, history, now)`: goals part first, then history rows.
- **Marker:** `SyncFile.GoalYearsMerged` (JSON `goalYearsMerged: [{year, rolledAt}]`, lenient, QR strips it), `SyncGoalYearMerged`.
- **For GS3 (App):** pass `DeviceId` and `DailyStepsService.Current` into `GoalSourceData`; write FocusLocal/Calendar/screen-time values through `GoalDerivedWriter`; `BeeminderEntries.Prune` drops entries locally (no tombstone) and must follow `MayChange` once synced; `GoalStore` keeps entry tombstones for good; set `lastMergeFrom[device]` after a regular merge and after a full history read; merge history only with or after the goals part.
- **Checks:** Core 1019/1019 (870 + 149 `gs1b/...`: rows, read, counting 22, writer 13, merge 6 x both ways x again, window, month, to-read (+ every listing order), roll-up (+ every month order), may-remove, roller, marker, codec, converge = 24 orders + all at once + again, stale, size). GoalsChecks 247/247, AppChecks 628/628, solution build 0 errors (6 old App warnings). Fixtures `round2-fixtures/gs1b/` (13 files) from `tasks-ui-checks/Gs1bFixtures.cs` + `Gs1bHistory.cs` + `Gs1bChecks.cs`; `Gs1aParts` now writes rows (gs1a fixtures regenerated; two GS1a string-replace checks adapted; backups `*.gs1bbak`). Mutations (`gs1b_mutations.py`): HC summed -> 2 fail; non-writer tombstoning -> 3 fail; rows to whole seconds -> 12 fail; entry tombstones pruned -> 6 fail; all restored. Logs `tasks-ui-logs/gs1b-*.log`.
- **Size (3 y x 20 goals x 3 devices, 65,700 entries):** row 111 B derived / 102 B hand (object 247 B). All as rows 7.02 MB JSON / 0.94 MB sealed (was 12.65 / 1.86). Regular file, 90 days (5,400): 0.60 MB / 0.079 MB. Month file (1,860): 0.198 / 0.029 MB. Year file (21,900): 2.33 / 0.381 MB. All far under the 16 MB guard and the relay's 4 MiB.

### R7.2 review fixes

- **Scope:** Android lane only (review of `bb360c4a`, `round2-deliverables/r72-review-fixes.md`): SHOULD 1-5 and every NIT; `doc/hard-rules.md` (the ADR 76 bullet's phone part, and the phone twin of the PC's Tasks layout, Q627). No new permission, sync field, dependency or schema change; `essentials/*` and the watch untouched. Must land before any phone build reaches the owner.
- **Pure decisions, JVM-checked (`shared/tasks/`):** `TaskModelStartup` (`unreadableOldStores`, `migrate` over a `TaskMigrationPort`, `open` -> `TaskStoreOpening` Open / Closed, `committed` = `PhoneTaskStore.commit`'s rule, `facadeChanges` = `SharedListsStore.edit`'s hand-over, `listsOf`), `TaskOldStore`, `TaskMigrationPort`, `TaskStoreOpening`, `TaskListUpdateNotice`, `TaskAddSent`, `TaskNumbering.groupNumberingDevice`. The app classes only read and write prefs around them.
- **1 an unreadable old store:** `migrate` first checks every old store that holds something (`shared_lists/lists` object, `task_categories/list` and `task_filter_presets/list` arrays): not opened by its key or not that JSON -> its sealed value is copied once into `task_model` (`unreadable-old-<prefs/key>`, never overwritten: the old screens, still the source, read such a store as empty and overwrite it at the next edit, as before R7.2), logged by name only, no backup, no migration, no marker; every start tries again.
- **2 an unreadable task list:** marker set and `lists` unreadable (or missing, or `load` throws) -> `PhoneTaskStore.isClosed` (like the PC's `Unreadable`): empty, no edits (`SharedListsStore.edit` keeps the four lists, `TaskCategoriesStore` drops edits), nothing sent (`nextTasks` / `parked` / `later` / `autoSaved` / `categories` / `taskParts` null, never empty), handovers wait unreceipted, no task-lists auto-backup, synced settings without `tasks`, a task restore refused, Tasks shows one notice, Today no task sections. The sealed value is set aside as `lists.unreadable` only when none is (never overwritten); at a start that can read `lists`, a readable set-aside copy is merged in (`TaskListMerge.mergeLists`; its newer-app entries kept only where the current list doesn't name their ids) and removed in the same commit (an R7.2 build's earlier copy is recovered this way). No Rebuild on the phone yet (Q640, R7.4).
- **3 release order "PC first":** a phone on the task list ignores a pre-ADR-76 PC's task edits (Q583); the owner's PCs are 0.1.131+ and write `tasks`, so **update every PC before the phone**. While a PC's file shares the old lists without `tasks` the phone says "Update AccountabiliBuddy on <PC>: its task edits don't reach this phone until it does." (a quiet line under the Today strip; an info banner on the old pane), only with "Sync task lists" on.
- **4 backup checked:** `recordBackup` returns the `commit()` result; false -> logged, no migration rule, no marker. **NIT stale backup:** the sealed copies are taken fresh on every attempt (same key per version), the folder backups once per version.
- **5 numbering:** `groupNumberingDevice(joined, received, self)`: unjoined or nothing received -> null (temporary numbers); a PC among the received files -> the lowest PC; otherwise (a file received, none a PC's) the phone rule. Used by `CompanionApp` for the store and the migration (Q599 updated by Q643).
- **NITs:** `TodayHandoff.answered` compares the Later item's title / note / open steps / estimate with what the `taskAdd` request carried (`TaskAddSent`; unreadable request -> the old stamp test); `PhoneTaskStore.mergeFile` keeps a newer app's entries that changed even when the lists didn't (written with them) and drops `fileCache` so they go out; `task_filter_presets` sentence in `doc/hard-rules.md` (Q605); no `TaskLocalEdits` parity fixture (the PC's `ApplyLocal` pairs older apps' moves on purpose, Q646).
- **Downgrade, then upgrade again (owner, read before installing an older phone build):** the marker stays set. An older build (before R7.2) shows and edits the old stores as they were at the switch (later task edits are not in them) and sends its edits as old fields, which a PC (0.1.131+) reads into its task list. Back on this build the phone reads its task list and **ignores what the older build wrote into the old stores**: edits made while downgraded reach the phone again only through a PC's file. In a phone-only group they are lost. An R7.2 build's own `lists.unreadable` is merged back at the first start of this build that can open it.
- **Checks:** Kotlin `:checks:run --offline` **1822/1823** (1783 + 40 `r72-fix`, a self-check against fakes of the prefs: old stores 4, migration 8, opening 8, numbering 7, notice 4, Add to Today 5, store edits 4). The one FAIL, `gs0/keep`, is not this lane's: it fails the same way on a clean checkout of `50aff818` (GS1b; 1782/1783, `r72fix-head-baseline.log`; Q647). Mutations (each failed, then restored): an unreadable old store migrated anyway -> 4 fail; a second failure overwrites `lists.unreadable` -> 3 fail; a backup commit result ignored -> 1 fail. `assembleDebug :wear:assembleDebug assembleRelease --offline` green; gradle stopped. Logs `tasks-ui-logs/r72fix-*.log`. No device.
- **Hand test:** the R7.2 list above, (1)-(11); plus (12) on a phone build of this commit, `tasks-model.log` after an update says `task list read` (no `could not be read`), and with a PC still on an older version the Tasks page shows "Update AccountabiliBuddy on <PC>".

### GS1b review fixes
- **Scope:** C# Core only (review of 50aff818, items 1-10). ADR 79 owner paragraph: two sentences (month rewrite rule; `goalYearsMerged` now `{year, rolledBy, rolledAt}`). No App, no Kotlin, no transport, no dependency, no schema change.
- **1 (blocker) closed months written:** `GoalHistoryRules.MonthToWrite` has no open-month gate: any month whose content differs from this device's last written version is written (at most daily per file; the closing write as before); first run = every month in `MonthsOf(held)` (new). `WritableMonths/IsWritable` renamed `OpenMonths/IsOpen` (only `MayRemoveMonth` uses it: a month file of an open month is never removed). A removed month written again is simply rolled up again (`RollUp` takes newer piece versions).
- **2 orphan rows re-read:** `GoalSyncMerge.MergeReported` -> `GoalSyncMergeResult(Lists, DroppedEntries)` (entries whose goal neither input holds; `Merge` = `.Lists`). `GoalHistoryRules.Merge(held, file?, IReadOnlyList<GoalHistoryRows(File listing, Entries)>, now)` -> `GoalHistoryMerged(Lists, Read, Dropped, GoalsPartMerged)`: `Read` = files to record in `lastRead` (a file with a dropped row is left out, so the next `ToRead` returns it again); `StillNewToGroup(newToGroup, merged)` = stays true until a merge had a goals part. GS3: record `lastRead` only from `Read`; set `lastMergeFrom` after a full read only when every file of it is in `Read`.
- **3 roller in key + marker:** `GoalHistoryFileKey.ForYear(year, rolledBy)` (DeviceId = rolling PC); `GoalHistoryYear.Key` uses it. `SyncGoalYearMerged(Year, RolledBy, RolledAt)` JSON `{year, rolledBy, rolledAt}`; an item without `rolledBy` reads as the empty id and matches nothing (`Marked` drops it). `Marked` keeps one item per (year, rolledBy). `MayRemoveMonth` matches year + rolledBy + rolledAt >=.
- **4 roll-up only adds:** `RollUp(existing, months, year, rolledBy, listing, now)`: null when `existing` is null and the listing has a year file of that year. On top of another PC's file the result is this PC's own key, every entry kept.
- **5 newer derived row without `i`:** `GoalEntryRows.Stamps` rebuilds the id from g/s/w/d when `s` is a known derived code (shared `DerivedId`), so `GoalRawItem` windows, files (`MonthContent`, `MonthsOf`) and merges it by id.
- **6 non-finite readings:** `GoalDerivedWriter.Changes` skips NaN/infinite readings; that day's own entry is left as is (not tombstoned by the cover); the last reading per day decides (a NaN after a finite one skips the day).
- **7 screen time:** documented (a device writes screen time derived or, before GS1b, without a device; both for one day only across the upgrade); when a day has a counted device's derived entry, no-device entries don't count; otherwise they still do (existing case kept).
- **8 steps:** `SyncSteps` days are added as one more Health Connect "device" (DeviceId = empty id) and HC takes the highest, so a real 0 from one device never hides 12000. Changed the old case "a day with an HC entry reads it, never the synced count" -> highest (owner rule).
- **9 -0.0:** rows write `"v":-0` / `"p":-0` for a negative zero (positive 0 still left out), read back -0.0 bit for bit. Kotlin: write `-0` when `value == 0.0 && 1 / value < 0`.
- **10 active device:** `GoalHistoryRules.ActiveDevices(IEnumerable<GoalGroupDevice(Id, IsPc, Removed, LastRegularMerge, YearsMerged)>, self, now)`: self unless removed + every non-removed device whose regular sync file was merged within 90 days (`ActiveWithin`), ordered by id text. `ForgetRemoved(lastMergeFrom, removed)`; `ToRead(..., lastMergeFrom, removed, self, ...)`: a removed device is never stale (its files read only when changed; it doesn't trigger year re-reads). Logged as a question.
- **Checks:** Core 1058/1058 (1019 + 39 new; changed `gs1b/...` cases updated), GoalsChecks 247/247. Fixtures `round2-fixtures/gs1b/` regenerated, 16 files (new: `active`, `history-before-goal`, `newer-derived-row`; changed: month-write, to-read (+`removed`), roll-up (+`listing`), may-remove, converge (files with keys, 5 inputs x 120 orders + next-sync re-read + all at once), counting, writer (readings may be "NaN"/"Infinity"), rows, rows-read). Mutations (`gs1bfix_mutations.py`): closed-month gate back -> 2 fail; file with dropped rows recorded as read -> 4 fail; marker ignores the rolling PC -> 2 fail; roll-up ignores the listing -> 1 fail; all restored. AppChecks 679/679 (628 + the PC lane's new checks); solution build 0 errors (a first try hit a .pdb lock held by another process, the retry was clean). Logs `tasks-ui-logs/gs1bfix-*.log`. Backups `*.gs1bfixbak`.
- **GS2 (Kotlin) must mirror:** all of 1-10, the API shapes above and the fixtures; `goalYearsMerged` items carry `rolledBy`; history file names (GS3/GS4) include the rolling PC for year files.
- Questions 648-650.

### PG1 done (PC Tasks columns page instead of scrolling)
- **What the owner sees:** Tasks grouped by Bins, Label or Tag never scrolls sideways. Columns share the card's width, at least 300 each (the three bins may shrink to 220 first, as before, so they still fit 901 x 567). When they don't fit, as many whole columns as fit show (1440: 4, 1100: 3, 901: 2 for 7 or 9 columns) and the rest are pages. A page bar sits under the columns (only when there is more than one page): previous / next chevrons, a dot per page (current one a bar), "3 / 9" past 7 pages. Last page keeps the same column width (left-aligned). The page is remembered per grouping for the session (memory only, `ColumnPagerViewModel`, not stored, not synced); a resize keeps the column the page was turned to in view (the anchor index is never rewritten by a resize).
- **Keys:** Ctrl+PageUp / Ctrl+PageDown on the Tasks page (nothing else on the page used them; the noise popover's volume keys and the hotkey box are elsewhere); Tab out of a page's last column goes to the next page's first column header (Shift+Tab to the last column of the page before), skipping the bar. Ctrl+Alt+Left/Right or the grip moving a column across a page edge turns to the page that holds it (`Pager.Reveal`, used once by the next rebuild, only when the order really changed). A task row or a column grip dragged over the bar's chevrons for 600 ms turns the page, and again every 600 ms while held (`Controls/PageTurnHover`; stops at the end).
- **Swipe (NAV2):** the Back / Forward swipe stays OFF over paged columns (`HorizontalScrollGuard` now also blocks over a `TaskColumnsPanel` that is paged); paging is by the bar and the keys only, not the swipe.
- **Animation:** a page turn slides 36 px and fades in over 170 ms x Settings > Animations (Off: nothing touched).
- **Files:** new `Services/Tasks/TaskColumnPaging.cs` (pure numbers), `ViewModels/ColumnPagerViewModel.cs`, `Controls/PageTurnHover.cs`; rewritten `Controls/TaskColumnsPanel.cs` (FirstIndex, ColumnsPerPage, hides off-page columns with IsVisible; pointer capture of a drag handle survives its column being hidden: tried in the harness); `Views/ShelfView.axaml(.cs)` (no ScrollViewer, page bar, TurnPage, Tab handler); `Views/TasksView.axaml.cs`, `Views/TaskSectionView.axaml.cs`, `Views/TaskColumnView.axaml.cs` (drag hooks), `ViewModels/ShelfViewModel.cs` (`Pager`), `ViewModels/TasksViewModel.cs` / `.Columns.cs`, `Services/Navigation/HorizontalScrollGuard.cs`; `doc/hard-rules.md` (new bullet in The PC's Tasks layout).

One earlier AppChecks run showed 4 `m5t4` session checks failing twice under machine load (other agents building), then 679/679 on a quiet rerun: flaky timing, not PG1. GoalsChecks 247/247. Core program 1056/1058: the 2 failures are `gs1b/may remove` lines in another agent's in-progress Core edit (SyncGoalYearMerged), not touched here. Solution/App build 0 errors (Core was briefly unbuildable from another agent's edit; retried). Logs `tasks-ui-logs/pg1-*.log`.
- **Render harness** (out of repo): new views `tasks-label6` (6 labels + No label) and `tasks-tag8` (8 tags + No tag; extras seeded by `SampleWorld.PagingExtras`, run them in their own process), `CapExp.cs` experiment (HARNESS_CAPEXP=1). tasks-label6 / tasks-tag8 in 6 combos at 1440 x 880 and 901 x 567: fit 12/12 each size. Existing tasks, tasks-label, tasks-none, shelf, tasks-session, tasks-session-shelf at both sizes: fit 36/36 each, menus 10/10; every exit code 0 (no Tasks 901 failures). Shots: `%TEMP%/claude/round2-shots-pg1` (1440, paged), `round2-shots-pg1-901`, `round2-shots-pg1-existing`, `round2-shots-pg1-existing-901`. Not drawn: hover, focus, the slide, a real drag.

### GS3a done (PC goals sync wiring, part 1)
- **Scope:** ADR 79 on the PC, part 1: the goal lists travel (App + one pure Core rule). Not done (GS3b): derived-value writing (tags extras, calendar, screen time), month/year history files on Drive/relay and the 25-day relay re-upload, wiring the Q649 pruned-goal-id set, `goalYearsMerged` writing. No dependency, connection, permission, schema or stored-key change beyond the optional fields below.
- **Out / in** (new `Services/Sync/GoalListSync.cs`): `Decorate` = the store's lists (`GoalStore.ToSync`, newer items kept) with Deep work composed (`GoalDeepWorkSync.Compose`, EFFECTIVE weekly / Track time only with their own stamp), `GoalSyncWindow.Apply` (90 days), `SyncGoalParts.Into`; no part at all while the goals aren't ready. `ReceiveAsync` = `GoalSyncMerge.MergeReported(held composed, SyncGoalParts.From(file))` -> `GoalsService.ApplySyncedAsync` (gated): Deep work split (`GoalDeepWorkSync.Split`), the received weekly target through the rule, the stored record keeps its own weekly-part stamp, then `GoalStore.ApplySyncedAsync` (NOT `ImportAsync`): taken as merged (no restamp, no unknowns brought back), lists + Deep work documents in one transaction, ONE `Changed`. `SyncCoordinator`: `GoalParts` / `MergeGoals` under `ShareTaskLists()` (receive also behind the 60 s future guard); the kept copy strips the five parts + `goalYearsMerged` (`SyncCoordinator.KeptCopy`, static); `SyncFileBuilder.ForQr` already stripped them (verified). App: store/service `SelfId` / `Self` = the group id (empty before a group), `goalStore.Changed` -> `NotifyHistoryChanged`, `goalData.DeviceId` + `Steps` (`dailySteps.Current`, recompute on its change).
- **Deep work rule** (`GoalsService.Received`): `DeepWorkGoalSettings` gains `Stamp` (in `goals.deepWork`), `PendingStamp`, `PendingAfterSession` (in `goals.deepWork.pending`; all null-omitted). Same values -> stamp adopted (a received pending dropped); higher / new / Track time only off -> at once (also in a session); lower / removed / Track time only on -> AB: held in a session (`PendingAfterSession`, rebased to session end + delay on the first tick after it), else `GoalLowering.Decide` with THIS PC's delay from its trusted-clock receipt time and the work-hours rule; PB: at once (work-hours rule kept, `MadeInProductaBuddy`). Lands with the sender's stamp. Cancel of a received one: `Stamp` = a new stamp after the received one, so the sender takes it as a raise. Local applies stamp `NewStamp` (now, after own / pending stamps, by self). The same file read again keeps the wait's time.
- **Store:** every local change restamps goals (`Normalize` Local: `GoalUnknowns.Keep` then `GoalEdits.Restamp` per touched goal, `GoalEdits.New` for an unstamped new one; an untouched or unchanged unstamped goal stays unstamped, so no-op changes still save nothing); the tidy move stamps the Area part with `GoalAreaTidy.StampId` (same as the merge); entry tombstones never pruned; `ChangeAsync(..., stamped: true)` for backups. `DeleteGoalAsync` -> `GoalTombstones.Delete` (entries / reminders untouched; the reminder source and counting already skip non-live goals). Reviews: new record id `GoalReviewIds.For(week)`, answers set `DecidedAt`.
- **Backups:** `GoalsExport.DeepWork` (`{"weeklyMinutes"}`, the phone's shape; new `Backup/GoalsBackupDeepWork.cs`); restore: Replace = every part of the backup's live goals stamped now by this PC (syncs out, wins), Merge = changed parts restamped; deletions never taken; `deepWork` through `RestoreDeepWorkAsync` (lower waits, refused in a session); `BackupAccess.Goals(store, goals, enforcing)` refuses a goals restore in a focus session the user started. A phone-shaped part restores here (checked).
- **Beeminder (needed once entries travel):** `Prune(lists, active, hasToken, now)`: nothing without a token (another PC's values stay); with one, unlinked entries of live goals become tombstones (a deleted goal's stay hidden). `Apply`: a deleted datapoint is a tombstone; a read revives a tombstoned day. **Behaviour change:** removing the token no longer removes the entries (Q654); checks `bm1/token` and `bm1fix/apply` adapted.
- **UI:** Edit goal › sources: a Beeminder row on a PC without a token says "Read on the PC that has your Beeminder token; its daily values reach this PC when it syncs"; Health Connect sleep "...Its daily values count here once your phone syncs them." Screen time text and the greyed device picker unchanged (ST2). Render: `goal-edit` at 901 x 567 without a token (new harness env `HARNESS_BEEMINDER_NOTOKEN=1`, `HARNESS_DIALOG_SIZE=901x567`), 6 combos, the row's text wraps, nothing clipped: `%TEMP%/claude/round2-shots-gs3a-901/goal-edit/` (harness backup `GoalDialogs.cs.gs3abak`).
- **Core (pure, Q649 proposal 1):** `GoalHistoryRules.Merge(..., IReadOnlySet<Guid> prunedGoals)` overload (rows of a pruned goal never keep a file unread; the old overload = empty set) and `GoalHistoryRules.PrunedGoalIds(before, after)`. **GS2 must mirror both** (no parity fixture yet).
- **Docs:** `doc/hard-rules.md`: sync-file field list gains the five parts + `goalYearsMerged`; ADR 71 bullet (one backup shape; the switch covers task and goal parts); Goals on the PC (no longer "device-local until ADR 79"; tombstone, delete and restore rules); BM1 (entries travel, token rule); goal reminders (reminders travel, marks never); review ids; new bullet "Goals sync on the PC (ADR 79, GS3a)". Phone bullets untouched (GS4).
- **Checks:** solution build 0 errors. AppChecks **724/724** (679 + 45 `gs3a/...` in the new out-of-repo `AppChecks/Gs3aChecks.cs`, hooked at the end of `Program.cs`, backup `Program.cs.gs3abak`): stamps on new goals; first sync of two devices (one Work, one Deep work, two same-named goals, identical store JSON, B's Deep work note + A's weekly); one event per applied merge (+ reminders re-plan); fixed point after two exchanges; file parts present, no `pendingChange`; sealed at rest after a merge (raw documents); restamp only the changed part; rename on A + pause on B both survive; delete keeps entries / reminders, deletion travels; raise in a session at once; lower waits B's 15-min delay from B's receipt, Target tile pending, old value / stamp out, no ping-pong while waiting, lands with A's stamp; held in a session and waits from its end; PB at once in a session; PB + work-hours held; Cancel re-stamps and A raises; Track time only on waits / off at once; incoming pending ignored, own pending never out, daily target untouched; tidy to Work with `StampId` and identical lists; entry tombstone kept after 40 days; review id + `decidedAt`; NotMigrated and Unreadable refuse and send no part; a file without parts leaves goals; absence never deletes; Beeminder token rule; backups (Replace stamps and wins on another device, deletion not taken, phone-shaped `deepWork` waits, refused in a session, export shape); a real `SyncCoordinator` (switch off: no parts out, none in; back on: merged, one Work / Deep work; kept copy `sync.received` raw bytes hold no part, name or marker; QR none; PC store sealed); guards grep; no goal text in any log. ### R7.3b done (phone Tasks drag, drop zones, picker, toast, Add)

- **What the owner sees on a phone:** a **hold of 450 ms anywhere on a closed row** (title too) lifts it: a haptic tick, the row stays at 35%, a tilted 250 dp card with its label dot and detail line follows the finger, the Today strip lights up ("Drop to add to **Today · Queued**"), 58 dp drop zones slide in over the card's edges (the neighbour pages; label-coloured when grouped by label, the card then reads "ePT → mPT · #Release"), and the tabs become "Hold to pick a bin" + a red **Cancel** ("keeps selected"). Hold ~300 ms on the left zone: a picker of every page above the bar (3 columns, "You are here", "No label"). Hold ~600 ms on a zone: the page turns. Near the list's edges it scrolls. Let go: strip → Today · Queued (never This session), zone / tile → that page (bin, label replaced, tag added), own list → a new place in its bin (Your order only), Cancel → nothing, selection kept; **let go without moving → select mode** with that row (× · "2 selected ⌄" · "Select all 4", bulk bar Move · Options · Delete; a hold on a selected row lifts the stack). An open task has no lift: a hold opens its ⋮ sheet, its title renames. Every move: the board's **toast** (Undo, ×, 3 dp timer bar, tap → that page with the rows flashing). A tick keeps the row struck through until its toast goes, then it collapses. "This session is full" sheet (swap / Add anyway with a reason). The open task: "Steps · 1/3 · add up to 35 min of 50", Add a step, a step menu (Make it its own task, Move up / down, Delete step). The **Add sheet** of the board: full height at once with the keyboard up and no jump, @label / #tag tinted and as chips with ×, + Time limit, + Reminder (due day, time, reminder rules), + Steps, + Note, Send to grid, Add another. **"Update AccountabiliBuddy on:"** is right now (decided from the mark the kept copy keeps), a line per PC, × for 3 days.

- **App (`ui/tasks/page/`):** `TasksPageHost` rewritten around one page gesture (`ShelfDragGesture` + `ShelfDragState`), `DragGhost`, `DropZone`, `DragBar` + `DragPicker`, `SelectMode` (`SelectHeader`, `BulkBar`), `BulkSheet`, `SessionFullSheet`, `TasksToastCard` (+ `TasksToast` fields), `TaskUpdateNoticeCard` (+ `rememberTaskUpdateNotice`, also on the old pane), `AddTaskSheet` (was `QuickAddSheet`), `ShelfRow` (`ShelfRowState`: lifted, selecting, selected, finishing, flash; TalkBack actions), `ShelfPageCard` (rows / list state / bounds, `animateItem`), `ShelfPager` (`EdgeZone`), `OpenTask` (`StepEdits`, rings line), `ShelfController` (reorder, swap, Add anyway, bulk, steps, own task, toasts); `ui/shell/BottomBarOverride` + `AppScaffold` (the tabs' place while dragging); `ui/sheets/AbSheet` `aboveKeyboard`; `sync/SyncCoordinator` (the tasks mark); `tasks/TasksUiState` + `TasksUiPrefs` (notice close, device ids only). No new permission, sync field, dependency or schema change; `essentials/*` and the watch untouched.
- **`doc/hard-rules.md`:** two bullets under "The phone's Tasks layout": drag / select / Add sheet rules, and the update notice. They went in with `7905c676` (GS3a), whose commit took the whole file while they were in the shared worktree; this commit has no hard-rules change.
- **Checks:** C# Core check program **1060/1060** (writes `r73/typed-title`, `r73/reorder`, `r73/toast-timing` from `tasks-ui-checks/R73bFixtures.cs`, hooked in `Round2Fixtures.WriteAll`; backup `Round2Fixtures.cs.r73bbak`). (2) Hold and drag to the strip → "1 task added to Today · Queued", Undo puts it back at its place; Today shows it under Queued, never This session. (3) Drag to the right zone → Later; hold the zone instead → the page turns. (10) Add to This session with it full → the "This session is full" sheet; swap → toast, Undo. (11) Open a task with steps: Add a step; a step's ⋮ → Make it its own task → it shows right below, Undo. (12) With every PC on 0.1.131+ the "Update AccountabiliBuddy on" notice is gone; with one older PC it lists that PC; × hides it; it is back 3 days later. (13) TalkBack: a row's actions offer "Move to Today · Queued", the neighbour pages, Move up / down, Select. (14) Motion off (Settings): zones fade in, no slides.

### GS3a review fixes
- **1 (blocker, ADR 84):** `BeeminderEntries.Apply(..., self, now)` writes `GoalEntry.DeviceId = self` (none when the PC has no device id yet; a read that changes a day takes the entry over). `Prune(lists, active, self, now)` (the `hasToken` flag is gone: no token = no active links) tombstones only live Beeminder entries of live goals that no Read link owns AND whose `DeviceId` is this PC's or none (pre-GS3a, never synced). Another PC's are never removed; a PC with a token revives its days on its next read. Services pass `_store.SelfId()`. `doc/hard-rules.md` BM1 bullet says so.
- **2 (ADR 48):** area, entry (own id and the day's other hand entries), reminder, review save and delete take `SharedListMerge.NextStamp(now, held)` inside the store change (`GoalsService.Tomb` for the tombstones); `GoalReviewService.DecideAsync` stamps `DecidedAt` after the held answer for that goal.
- **3:** `GoalsService.ApplySyncedAsync(Func<GoalSyncLists?> merge, source)`: `GoalListSync.ReceiveAsync` hands over the held-and-merge step, which runs INSIDE the gate right before `GoalStore.ApplySyncedAsync` (nothing awaited between; a change saved from another thread in that instant is the only gap left). `Received` returns early when the received Deep work stamp is not newer than this PC's (time, then device id as in the merge).
- **NITs:** (a) `GoalsBackupDeepWork.TrackTimeOnly` (optional bool; a PC backup always writes it): restore keeps it; a phone-shaped part leaves this PC's alone (the "off" step is skipped when the part has no `trackTimeOnly`). (b) `GoalStore.Normalize` (Local and Stored) drops entries and reminders of a goal whose tombstone it prunes in that pass. (c) not changed, see Q712. (d) Q713.
- **Checks:** build 0 errors. AppChecks 780/780 (724 + 6 new `gs3afix/...` [stamps, skew, gate, stale-weekly, trackonly, pruned-goal] + the old `gs3a/beeminder` replaced by `gs3afix/beeminder-owner` + 50 `td1/...` from the PC lane's file in the same project); `bm1/token` changed to expect this PC's own entries removed. Mutations (`tasks-ui-checks/gs3afix_mutations.py`, logs `tasks-ui-logs/gs3afix-mutation-*.log`): each fails alone (779/780): Prune without the ownership test -> `beeminder-owner`; merge before the gate -> `gate`; stale-weekly guard removed -> `stale-weekly`. All restored. Core 1060 and GoalsChecks 247 run next (see report). Questions 710-713.

Backups: export carries `trackTimeOnly` (the weekly in effect), restore through the same apply rules, refused in a session.

The 2 failing lines are `gs1b/writer` against fixture files written while the PC lane had a mutation in `GoalDerivedWriter.IsOwn` (Q760). Mutations: a lowered weekly applied at once in AB -> 11 fail; history merge on a snapshot taken before the gate -> 1 fail; both restored. The store's `@Synchronized` itself is Android code (no JVM check). `assembleDebug :wear:assembleDebug assembleRelease --offline` green. Logs `tasks-ui-logs/gs4-*.log`.
- **hard-rules text for the PC agent (doc/hard-rules.md, phone bullets):** "Goals on the phone (ADR 79): the phone's goal lists, Deep work's weekly target with Track time only and the entries it measures travel in its sync file under 'Sync task lists' (the five goal parts, never a waiting change, entries of the last 90 days; the rest in the sealed month/year history files on Drive and the relay, never in a QR file); the same merge, stamp, tombstone and restore rules as the PC. A weekly target or Track time only received from another device is a change like one made here: higher applies at once, lower or removed waits the phone's own looser-change delay from the receipt (trusted time), is held during a focus session outside ProductaBuddy and counted from its end, lands with the sender's stamp; Cancel stamps the phone's value again. The phone writes only its own Health Connect steps and sleep entries (device id on them) and never removes another device's or Beeminder's. Goal restores (any platform's backup) go through the same rules, are refused during a session, stamp now with Replace and never take deletions. A phone not in a group, or with the switch off, sends no goal part and takes none in. Goal names, notes and reasons stay private text, sealed at rest and never logged; history passes log counts only."
- **Owner two-device hand test (PC + phone, same group, Drive or relay):** (1) both on, both open Goals: one Work, one Deep work, same-named goals stay two. (2) Tick a habit on the phone: the PC shows it within a sync. (3) Rename a goal on the PC and change its colour on the phone: both survive. (4) Delete a goal on one: gone on the other, entries kept in History. (5) Raise Deep work's weekly target on the PC: the phone has it at once; lower it: the phone shows the wait (its own delay) and Cancel there makes the PC take the old value back; start a session on the phone and lower on the PC: it waits until the session ends plus the delay. (6) Phone steps goal: the PC shows the phone's steps. (7) Turn "Sync task lists" off on the phone: the PC stops getting its goals and the phone stops taking the PC's. (8) Restore a goals backup on the phone: it syncs out; in a session it is refused. (9) After a day: History files in the Drive folder (`.abgh`) and on the relay; leave the group on the phone: its relay files go.
- Questions 750-760.

### GS3b done (PC derived values + goal history files)
- **Scope:** ADR 79 on the PC, part 2 (App + sync/goals docs; Core untouched). No dependency, connection, permission or schema change; the only new stored thing is the sealed device-local document `goals.history.sync`.
- **Derived values** (`Services/Goals/GoalDerivedValuesService.cs`): through Core's `GoalDerivedWriter`, own id only. **#tag extra** = `GoalDerivedMinutes.FocusLocalExtra` over this PC's OWN sessions (`GoalSourceDataService.OwnFocus`, new); **calendar extra** = `GoalDerivedMinutes.CalendarExtra` over this PC's calendar windows (goal counts only, ADR 58; not written while this PC has no calendar window at all: no zeros over earlier values on the guess that the calendar isn't loaded). Last 90 days, only days that have own focus / calendar time / an own entry; run at most every 5 min (`goalData.Changed` + a timer), nothing outside a group or before the sessions are read. No churn (second run = 0 changes, stamp unmoved). Beeminder: unchanged, it already writes `DeviceId = self` (GS3a fixes); the writer's `MayChange` covers it (check). Screen time is ST2. `goalData.DeviceId` / `Steps` were already wired (GS3a).
- **History files** (`Services/Sync/GoalHistorySync.cs` + `IGoalHistoryStore`, `DriveGoalHistoryStore`, `RelayGoalHistoryStore`, `GoalHistoryNames`, `GoalHistoryState*`): one pass per transport (at most every 15 min): list -> read (`GoalHistoryRules.ToRead`, merge AFTER the regular files' goals part, `Merge(..., PrunedGoals)`) -> roll up (lowest-id active PC, from 1 Feb; reads first; a listed unreadable year file is never rolled over) -> read again (the roller merges and marks its own year) -> write own months (`MonthToWrite`: any changed month, daily, all on first run) -> remove (`MayRemoveMonth`: Drive deletes, relay = not put again) -> put again. **Names:** SHA-256 over `goalhist|<groupId>|<key text>` (`m|device|yyyy|mm`, `y|rolledBy|yyyy`), 12 hex + `.abgh` on Drive / `.absync` on the relay (the relay's only shape); the phone's `GoalHistoryNames.kt` already gives the same names (checked against my value). The relay's regular reader skips history files by known name and by the `ABM1`/`ABY1` header. **Relay:** own files put again every 25 days (months re-sealed from the last written version, year files passed through byte for byte); never deletes outside leaving (own history names go with the sync file on leaving / rekey); a file over 4 MiB is not written; the 64-file limit keeps 8 places for sync files. **State** (`goals.history.sync`, DPAPI-sealed, reset on a new key, Drive's old files deleted then): per transport files read (+ etag), last written months, uploaded times, removed months, slim year metas, own names; shared: `GoalsPartMerged` (new-to-group flag), `LastRegularMerge`, `LastMergeFrom`, per-device `goalYearsMerged` markers (stripped from the kept copy), own marker, tombstoned + pruned goal ids (Q649; `GoalHistoryRules.PrunedGoalIds` on every store change + a persisted diff for prunes while the app was off). `GoalListSync` gained `Held()`, `YearsMerged` (sets `goalYearsMerged` in the regular file) and `Merged` (-> `NoteRegularFile`). **Active device** = regular file WITH goal parts merged within 90 days (an older app that sends none neither rolls nor blocks removals).
- **Docs:** `doc/hard-rules.md` new bullet "Goal history files and derived values on the PC" + a clause in the first (Local only) bullet. Project `CLAUDE.md` line "Google Drive ... sealed sync files and sealed settings backups only" not touched (outside the lane, Q748).

Core **1060/1060**, GoalsChecks **247/247**. The 55: derived writer (tags extra value / id / device, no double count on the writer and on another PC, in-place update, real 0, others' entries / hand / Beeminder untouched, Core writer in a cover, calendar extra, calendar never in Deep work / focus windows / guards (grep), Beeminder path, no group = nothing), Drive history (opaque sealed names, write, read, daily rule, unchanged = no rewrite, late joiner: order with the goals part, Q649 pruned vs not-yet-arrived, stale device re-read), roll-up (not before 1 Feb, roller only, pieces, marker, kept copy has none, no removal before every active device merged, removal after, no rewrite of a removed month, late joiner gets the year), two rollers (two files, no loss, both marked), relay (names, 25-day re-upload with the relay's 30-day expiry, never deletes, removed month not put again, year passed through unchanged and not downloaded again, name recognition), size limit, 64-file headroom, leaving names / rekey, switch off, logs counts only. ### GS2 review fixes (Android lane, fix round after GS4)
- **Scope:** `android/shared` + `android/checks` only (nothing in `android/app`, `src/`, `doc/`). GS4's two items (newerSources kept, lower-case device id) were done there, not redone.
- **SHOULD 2, the "+" in a time:** the regex shortcut in `GoalJson.text` is gone. A time the app writes for a KNOWN DateTimeOffset field is `GoalJson.timeValue(t)` (the time text with a private mark, U+0001, that only the text writer reads and drops); every other string, unknown fields and newer items included, is written through the escape rule ("+" as \u002B), as .NET's encoder does. Readers drop the mark (`GoalJson.unmark`: `parseTime`, `text()`, `GoalRawItem`, `TaskJson.time`, `SyncFileJson`), `JsonParity` compares without it. A raw (newer) item the merge restamps and a decision's `decidedAt` are written as plain text, because the PC reads those items back as JSON before writing (so they come out escaped there too). All known-time writers (`GoalJson`, `GoalHistoryJson`, `GoalHistoryStateJson`, `GoalStampJson`, `GoalSyncJson`, `SyncFileJson`, `TaskJson`) use `timeValue`.
- **Checks compare the PC's raw bytes:** the C# fixture files re-escape every string (a time too), so they cannot tell a known time from a text. The C# writer now adds `expectedWire` (the PC's serializer output) to the cases whose result is serialized: gs1a merge, review-three, deep-work, deep-work-split, edits, tombstones, parts; gs1b rows (back), writer, merge, converge. `FixtureCase.wire(part)`, `Gs2Json.same(expected, actual, wire)` compare the phone's text with it; the old text-vs-text compare (the same writer on both sides) and the `plain()` undo-the-escape hack are removed. `r71-self` "a store read and written keeps ..." now compares with the store's raw text.
- **New fixture** `gs1a/wire-plus` (C#: `tasks-ui-checks/Gs2FixFixtures.cs`, written by `Round2Fixtures.WriteAll`, Core checks `gs2fix/wire` in `Program.cs`): two devices, SAME edit time, "+" and "-" offsets in unknown fields of a goal, area, entry, reminder, a newer goal (unknown kind) and a newer review decision; the merge's wire text. The goal case only passes with "+" (backslash sorts after "-"; a plain "+" would pick the other side). Backups `*.gs2fixbak`.
- **SHOULD 3 (phone side):** `optionalNumber`: NaN, Infinity, 1e400 (number or text) are damage (the entry / goal is left out); a text number is plain JSON form only (" 1.5" rejected, Q771). `literalText` throws instead of writing a bare NaN / Infinity (Q770). `GoalCounting.combined` (now `internal`): Health Connect / calendar highest by .NET's double order (-0 equals 0, a NaN is lowest, never chosen over a number).
- **SHOULD 4 perf:** `GoalListMerge.orderedByPosition` / `sortedByText` make each id's text once per item (merge order, `GoalSyncMerge.ordered`, `monthContent`, `mergeEntries`); `GoalListMerge.compare` returns 0 for an unchanged copy before any tie-break text. 65 000-entry timing in `gs2fix-self` (merge 136 ms, history 64 ms, rows text 273 ms here; limit 10 s each).
- **NITs:** offset-less times stay unsupported, logged (Q772). Tombstone with `"target": null` kept with the placeholder (`GoalSyncJson.readGoals`). `GoalHistoryJson`: names in any case, numbers as text, a null month piece dropped. `GoalHistoryRules.merge` lists a file once per (key, instant). Not machine-checked (Q796). **SHOULD 3:** `deepWork.onChanged` backs up AND `sendSyncFilesSoon()` (via `PhoneGoalSyncWiring.afterWeeklyChange`).
- **SHOULD 4:** `GoalHistorySync` has one `ReentrantLock` for passes and state changes, let go only around `applySynced` (a pass then checks its state is still current); cheap readers use a snapshot; the store's change hook POSTS `trackPrunedGoals` (Q791-793). **SHOULD 5:** `GoalHistorySync.isHistoryName` (the PC's `IsHistoryName`, cached candidates) lets the relay reader skip history files by name before any GET; the header fallback stays and `noteHistoryName` keeps what it finds in the state across restarts (Q794). **SHOULD 6:** `GoalApplyResult` (Changed / Unchanged / Failed) from `applyHistory`; reads are recorded unless Failed (Q798).

Shared: `TaskLabelColors.typed/withOpacity`, `TaskLabelEdits.checkName/useCount/setRingOn`, `TaskLabelWords`.

- **Not built / kept** (see checklist): the phone's Actions-in-a-session / Session view / Shelf group / default time limit / title help settings (no phone consumer), scrollbar, view menu Rename/Duplicate, Sync card, "Changed on both devices", the Tasks page opening with the account's default view, PC-style reminder volume/rising/dismiss settings and test rings (the phone sets no task alarm), snooze shortcuts, Reset of task settings, parity fixtures for the account-edit rules (PC lane exports them).
- Behaviour changes: Settings gets a Tasks page; with no PC a phone's Deep work daily target is editable; an unjoined phone's tasks get final numbers.

**Hard-rules text for the orchestrator (`doc/hard-rules.md`, NG1 + ADR 76/79):**
- Deep work's daily target: while a PC is in the phone's group it is the PC's, read only on the phone (ADR 29). With no PC in the group (or no group) it is the phone's own, kept in this phone's preferences, sent in no file; raising or adding applies at once, lowering or removing waits the looser-change delay from the trusted time, is refused and never applied during a session (ProductaBuddy: at once). When a PC joins, the PC's value always counts; the phone's own is kept unused and counts again if the PCs leave. Amends ADR 29 for groups without a PC.
- Shared-folder sync carries goal history files (`.abgh`, the same names and sealing as Drive), each device putting and deleting only its own.
- "Hide the Shelf during a session" turning off follows the looser-change rule (waits, never during a session); Rebuild of an unreadable task store is refused in a session and never deletes the set-aside copy.
- A phone with no group numbers its own tasks final; it renumbers only when it joins a group with a PC.

### GS3b review fixes
- **Scope:** `src/` (Core + App), `doc/hard-rules.md`, ADR 79 (two sentences, see Nits). No Android file touched. Checks out of repo (`tasks-ui-checks/AppChecks/Gs3bFixChecks.cs`, `Gs3bFixCoreChecks.cs`, `gs3bfix_mutations.py`; logs `tasks-ui-logs/gs3bfix-*.log`).
- **Blocker 1 (relay cap):** on the relay only the roller (lowest-id active PC) keeps finished months; others keep open months (current + previous UTC month); each device keeps at most the quota, newest first; 429 on a history put = group full, pass ends, no retry. Drive unchanged. `GoalHistorySync.WriteAsync` iterates months newest first.
- **Blocker 2 (late joiner):** `GoalHistoryRules.LatePrunable` + `Merge(..., GoalHistoryLatePrune)` (new overload, `GoalHistoryMerged.NewlyPruned`); state `FirstDropAt` is set at the first merge that dropped a row of an unpruned unknown goal, cleared when none are left.
- **Should:** (a) month re-put = GET then PUT same bytes (re-seal only if the file is missing); relay writes gated by `GoalHistoryRules.KnowsHistory` per non-removed device card (PC 0.1.134, phone 0.1.78 constants; Drive unaffected); (b) Drive: list first, then delete stale names; stale names kept in `GoalHistoryState.StaleNames`; `LeaveAsync` deletes own Drive history files when the PC has no key; (c) `GoalDerivedValuesService.InGroup` (App wires `syncIdentity.Key != null`); (d) vanished year file: `ForgetVanishedYears` clears `Removed` + the year's record so own months are put again and rolled again; (e) `IGoalHistoryStore.MaxRequestsPerPass` (relay 40, Drive none), pass stops with `capped=1`; (f) per-transport `NewToGroup` flag: reads every listed file once (own too), cleared after a pass with a goals part merged and every file read or skipped (`AllAccountedFor`). Also: nothing is downloaded before a goals part merged, unless this PC knows no other device (a lone PC never gets one: found by the 1-device sim, its marker never went out).
- **Nits:** newest months first; ADR 79 now says a month file holds every entry the device HOLDS for that month (what the code does, `MonthContent`); this breaks no rule (union by id, newest wins) and is what lets the single relay roller carry everyone's finished months. An unreadable / too-big file is logged and skipped and never blocks a stale device's complete read nor `NewToGroup`. hard-rules: relay retention, gate, late joiner, and "GS3a without GS3b would block removal, ship together".
- **Exact Core rules the phone must mirror** (all pure, fixtures in `%TEMP%/claude/round2-fixtures/gs3bfix/*.json`: relay-quota, relay-keep, knows-history, late-prunable, all-accounted):
  1. `RelayQuota(yearFiles, knownDevices) = max(0, floor((64 - 16 - max(0, yearFiles)) / max(1, knownDevices)))`; yearFiles = year files in the listing, knownDevices = devices not removed incl. self.
  2. `RelayKeep(months, isRoller, quota, now)`: distinct; if not roller keep only `OpenMonths(now)` (current UTC month and the one before); newest first; take max(0, quota). Candidates exclude months already removed. Months not kept are not put (not re-put either).
  3. `KnowsHistory(kind, appVersion)`: watch = true; pc >= 0.1.134, phone >= 0.1.78; leading digits/dots of the version parsed as a version, else false; unknown kind or null = false. Relay writes (month, year, re-puts) only while true for every non-removed device.
  4. `LatePrunable(dropped, firstDropAt, active, self, lastRegularMerge, alreadyPruned, now)`: [] unless firstDropAt != null and every ACTIVE device other than self has `lastRegularMerge > firstDropAt` (strictly); then each goal not already pruned whose dropped rows ALL have `now - stamp > 30 days`; sorted by id text. The merge treats those goals as pruned for the "file read" decision and adds them to the kept pruned set.
  5. `AllAccountedFor(listing, lastRead, skipped)`: every listed file is in skipped or has lastRead == its listed time.
  6. Relay put: a 429 ends the pass; request cap per pass 40 (list, get, put each count); month re-put = same bytes (GET own file, PUT it; re-seal only when missing).
  7. GoalHistoryDeviceInfo equivalent: kind + appVersion of each card.
- **Checks:** build 0 errors; AppChecks **858/858** (835 + 23 `gs3bfix/...`: fake relay with 64 files, 4 MiB, 30-day expiry, 429 on a new name when full, 120 requests a minute, own files only, put audit; steady state over 10 simulated years for 1 / 3 / 5 devices and 5 devices with one that never sends a year marker: <= 48 history files, <= 64 in all, no 429, <= 40 requests a pass, none over 120 a minute; late joiner as roller (quota, newest first) and as non-roller (<= 2); 429 ends the pass; request cap; gate; month re-put bytes; late-joiner marker; Drive stale/leave; vanished year; new-to-group; derived value without a group). Core **1099/1099** (1074 + 25 `gs3bfix/...`, fixtures regenerated), GoalsChecks **247/247**. Mutations (both fail as required; restored): no quota -> 3 fail (steady 5-with-blocker, quota, late-joiner); late-joiner rule off -> 1 fail (`late-joiner-marker`).

### MIG1 done (older tasks land in To sort) + Today sidebar click target
- **Rule** (`TaskMigration.cs` + `.kt`, same): every open task of nextTasks, Later, come-backs, auto-saved goes to To sort in list order (positions 1..n); next-session and Later ones get origin "From the earlier version" (`TaskOrigins.EarlierVersion` / `EARLIER_VERSION`), come-backs and auto-saved keep "Parked ..." / "Auto-saved ..."; finished unchanged (done today in nextTasks -> Finished, else History); counts still balance. `FillSession` kept (the Shelf edits use it), no longer called by the migration.
- **Reader** (`OldTaskFieldsReader`): a NEW item from another device's old fields lands in To sort (origin Parked / Added from phone / earlier version). Known items keep their bin. This PC's own list edits (ApplyLocal, sender = self) keep their list's bin.
- **Notice:** PC `EarlierTasksNoticeStore` (doc `tasks.earlierNotice`, count + dismissed), `EarlierTasksCardViewModel`, card on Today (`SessionViewModel.EarlierTasks`) and Tasks; hidden in a session; Sort them now opens the Tasks tab (To sort = first Shelf column). Phone: `PhoneTaskStore.earlierNotice` (prefs, written in the activate commit), `EarlierTasksCard.kt` on Today banner and Tasks page; Sort them now sets the To sort chip. What's new text gets a sentence on both. `hard-rules.md` has the rule.

### Phone Tasks bug fixes (owner testing: +, Undo label, hold)
- **"+" not at the right**: in `ShelfPageCard` the title (`weight(1f, fill=false)`) and the `Spacer(weight(1f))` split the free width half and half, so the "+" sat at the middle of the free space. Title and count are now one `Row(weight(1f))`; the "+" is the only thing after it.
- **Undo while grouped by Label**: data was fine (`ShelfEdits.restore` brings bin, place, label, tags, steps back). The page was the problem: Group by Label has one page per label *with a task*, so ticking a label's last task dropped its page, the pages shifted, the page showing fell back to the start page (`currentKey` null) or a neighbour, and the struck-through row had no page to draw on. Now the tick pins `currentKey`/`lastIndex`, and `ShelfPages.keepHeld` keeps the page (empty) at its old place while the toast shows. Check `phonebug1/undo:` (7) in `checks/PhoneBug1Check.kt`, called from `TasksDragSelfCheck.run` (not registered in Main.kt: it has the other agent's uncommitted edits).
- **Hold did nothing**: `shelfDrag` read each touch's events with `awaitPointerEvent(Main)` straight after the Initial-pass down, which delivered the down's own Main pass, where the row's tap handler had already consumed the down; the loop called the hold off on `isConsumed` every time. Now the hold watches the Initial pass and never reads "consumed": time, distance past the touch slop, finger up, decided by the pure `shared/tasks/ShelfHold` (hold -> lift; hold + release -> select; hold + move -> drag; quick move -> swipe/scroll; tap -> row). Checks `phonebug1/hold:` (6). Not seen on a device.
- **Title tap**: a closed row's title has no handler of its own in the code (only an open task's renames); the decision is now `ShelfTitleTap.of(expanded, selecting)` (checks `phonebug1/title:` 3). If the title still absorbs a tap on the device, the cause is not in `ShelfRow`; please tell me which page/grouping.

### SYNC1a done (sync receipts core)
- **Scope:** Core + Kotlin twins, pure + parity. No App wiring, no UI, no writing of receipts (SYNC1b/c). ADR 86 (Accepted) and `doc/hard-rules.md` (field list, rewritten "Sync receipts" bullet) updated.
- **File shape:** `SyncFile.Receipts` (`IReadOnlyList<SyncReceipt>?`, appended LAST, `SyncLenientListConverter`, omitted when null) / Kotlin `SyncFile.receipts` (written last by `SyncFileJson`, read by `SyncReceiptsJson`, by hand, R8-safe). `SyncReceipt(from, held, byMethod?, failed?, parts?, history?)`, `SyncPartReceipt(at, state)`, `SyncHistoryReceipt(month, version)`. Vocabulary + bounds + `Clean` in `SyncReceiptParts` (16 entries, 5 methods, 12 parts, 3 months; unknown codes dropped, duplicate `from` keeps the first, empty `from` dropped, months newest first). The clean runs inside `SyncFileCodec.TryOpen/Open` (C# and Kotlin). Stripped in `SyncFileBuilder.ForQr` and `SyncFileStrip.forQr`; the kept copy (`keptCopy`, and the PC's stored copy) leaves it alone. A bad entry (wrong-typed time / map, not an object) is dropped, the file opens; Kotlin matches C# case by case (parity fixtures).
- **Read failures:** `SyncFileCodec.TryOpen` -> `SyncFileOpenResult(File, Failure)`, `SyncFileOpenFailure` {Damaged, OtherGroup, TooBig, NewerFormat}; `.Code` = the receipt reason code. NewerFormat is told from the JSON's own `formatVersion` before the shape is read. `Open` = `TryOpen(...).File` (null for all, as before). Kotlin: `tryOpen`, `SyncFileOpenFailure` (with `.code`).
- **Colour rule** (`SyncReceiptBoard.Method/Part`, `SyncDot(color, reason)`; Kotlin twin): inputs `SyncSentFacts` (newest, when it reached each method, previous newest per method as of the receiver's last file), `SyncReceiverFacts` (kind, removed, newest writtenAt held, whether its file carries a receipts list, `seen`, its receipt for me, last read per method). SYNC1b/c must supply these: the sender has to remember when each of its files reached each method (`ReachedAt`, `PreviousSentAt`). Rules as in the plan check 2.5; skew 2 min; silent 30 days; quiet = 3 x 5 min for folder/Drive/relay only.
- **Receiver-local record:** `SyncReceiptsLocal` (C#) / Kotlin twin: `RecordArrival` (monotonic, clears that method's failure), `RecordFailure`, `RecordParts`, `RecordHistory`, `Forget`, `ForFile(self, active)` (null when alone). Pure; SYNC1b/c store it (PC document `sync.receipts.local`, phone private pref, in no backup). A failure before any file makes a row with `held` = year 1.
- **Keep both (phone):** `shared/tasks/TaskConflictActions.kt` (`resolveConflict`, `keepBothVersions`, `TaskConflictChange`), twin of `TaskActions.ResolveConflict/KeepBothVersions`; the caller must forget the task's Undo entries. The empty id is the all-zero UUID.
- **Checks (outside the repo, logs `tasks-ui-logs/sync1a-*.log`):** Core `Sync1aChecks.cs` -> 1192/1192 (1103 + 89); fixtures `sync1a/{receipts-file,board,local,keep-both}.json`; Kotlin `Sync1aCheck.kt` (in repo, registered in `Main.kt`, `sync1a` added to `fullyCheckedAreas`) -> 2617/2617 (2499 + 102 mine + 16 from other work in the tree); AppChecks 858 (see log); solution build 0 errors; `assembleDebug assembleRelease --offline --no-daemon` OK (needs `ANDROID_HOME`, no local.properties in this worktree). Mutations: receipts left in QR -> 1 Core fail and 1 Kotlin fail; red when the receiver simply has not written -> 6 Core fails (and the same 6 Kotlin fails against those fixtures).
- **Not done / for SYNC1b+c:** the old-reader check is a shape check (a reader without the field reads a file that has receipts; the real reader ignores unknown members), not a run of an old build. No check that no backup kind carries `receipts` (no backup writes a `SyncFile`; the local record stays out of them by design, to be asserted where it is stored). Questions 850-852.

### MIG1 review fixes
- **BLOCKER fixed (mixed-version groups).** `OldTaskFieldsReader` reverted to before MIG1 (no `isNew` To sort, no earlier-version origin): a new item from an older device keeps its list's bin. `TaskMigration` (C# and Kotlin twin) stamps every live task's Bin field and its `EditedAt` at the migration time (`max(editedAt, now)`; the other parts keep the old edit time), so `OldTaskFieldsWriter` leaves deletion markers in nextTasks/later/autoSaved for 30 days and an older device sees each task once. `EditedAt` had to move too: the reader treats a marker newer than the live copy's `EditedAt` as a delete.

Kotlin: two checks in `TaskModelSelfCheck` (stamps, markers). Mutation (bins stamped at the old edit time) -> 2 Core fails (stamp check, 950/951/952 twice).
- **SHOULD.** `EarlierTasksNoticeStore.RecordBlocking`/`DismissAsync` catch all (a notice failure never fails a migration whose marker is set); `tasks.earlierNotice` is in `DeviceSettingsRegistry.StateKeys`; render harness seeds the card (count 12), 901 x 567 renders in `tasks-ui-shots/mig1fix-901` fit (54/54).
